# Changelog

[한국어](CHANGELOG.ko.md).

## Unreleased

- T26.7-4-5: the rules of docs/spec/editor.md that bind only the TypeScript editor tools live in docs/operations/editor-tools.md, and the Python package does not export analyze_prefix (children T26.7-4-5-1 and -2). Red: `make text-absent FILE=docs/spec/editor.md TEXT="**EDT-1**"` fails at 91289159 (the rule was still in the spec); green: the editor rules are gone from the spec, the Python export test and docs-check pass (T26.7-4-5).
- T26.7-4-6: concurrent-bound-map cites what its tests check (RT-70, VAL-22), and every requirement it listed before is cited by a unit with real tests in every implementation: RT-1 runtime-operations, RT-37 the conformance suite, RT-55 level-absence, RT-56 runtime-tiers and artifact-store, RT-59 request-path, RT-62 prepared-render, RT-69 engine-program (children T26.7-4-6-1 to -7). Red: `make parity-declarations-check RULES=R13` exit 2 at 06bd60e5 (seven requirements cited by no unit); green: R13 and the Go and Python tests hold (T26.7-4-6).
- T26.7-31: every unit of config/parity.json cites only the requirements that the tests of its bindings name, and every test file is declared: the full parity check holds after 9fb62722 (the unit artifact-store binds the loadOrRefresh tests, and the Rust SHA-256 test is internal). Red: `make parity-declarations-check` exit 2 at 935efcc5 (R5 on six test files); green: it passes after 9fb62722 (T26.7-31).
- T26.7-4-6-7: RT-69 holds in every implementation: the interface test checks that the engine owns exactly the one program that the manifest names (tests a90f4131, unit engine-program 5ca5c4ce). Red: `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="engine-program"` fails at 5263c35d (no unit); green: it passes after 5ca5c4ce (T26.7-4-6-7).
- T26.7-4-6-6: RT-62 holds in every implementation: a prepared render of a loaded artifact renders identical bytes on every call and equals the single-call render (tests f6e0ad1b, unit prepared-render c17a6413). Red: `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="prepared-render"` fails at 1b945ddb (no unit); green: it passes after c17a6413 (T26.7-4-6-6).
- T26.7-4-6-5: RT-59 holds in every implementation: a loaded artifact renders without parsing or source discovery, twice with identical bytes and an unchanged assign (tests a7931455, unit request-path 0fcadd0e). Red: `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="request-path"` fails at ff75f36a (no unit); green: it passes after 0fcadd0e (T26.7-4-6-5).
- T26.7-4-6-4: RT-56 artifact-runtime holds in every implementation: loadArtifact loads the AST artifact of the build compiler, checks its manifest and every file digest, and renders it (TS 8eaea197, Go 07ff4ebd, Rust daad693e, PHP aebfe3c4, Python 0b4a2a3e, PHP extension 02e2a620); ArtifactStore.loadOrRefresh applies the dev, true and false policies (TS d4132d7f, Go bbd9711c, Rust 92893fd2, PHP a3b70d57, extension b8a713b0, Python 0176747f); the unit runtime-tiers binds them (e21a31c1). Red: `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="runtime-tiers"` fails at 4efd8fa7 (no unit); green: it passes after e21a31c1 (T26.7-4-6-4).
- T26.7-32-23: concurrent-renders binds RT-70 in TypeScript, Rust, PHP and the PHP extension, each with a test that renders one program concurrently with its own data. Red: `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="concurrent-renders" RULES=R2` exit 2 at 4e375d39 (no binding); green: that check holds after 3d4eae64, and the Rust concurrent-renders test passes (T26.7-32-23).
- T26.7-32-21: concurrent-bound-map binds RT-70 and VAL-22 in TypeScript, Rust, PHP and the PHP extension, each with a concurrent test of one bound map. Red: `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="concurrent-bound-map" RULES=R2` exit 2 at 97f9d9ad (rust, ts, php, php-ext had no binding and Rust was not Send); green: that check holds after b61496fa, and `cargo --test concurrent_bound_map` passes after 7b36807b (T26.7-32-21).
- T26.7-32-21 (Rust public API change): the Rust library is Send and Sync for RT-70: `Rc` became `Arc`, the template cache `RefCell` became `Mutex`, and `Loader`, `HostFunction` and `TemplateObject` require `Send + Sync`; a host with a non-Send loader or function must change it.
- T26.7-4-6-3: RT-55 states the levels and the absent operations (spec ebacb176), and binds through the unit level-absence to a test in each implementation that asserts the absence (PHP and extension, TS, Go, Rust, Python). Red: `make parity-declarations-check RULES=R13 IDS="RT-55"` exit 2 at 2f05d06e; green: that check and `UNITS="level-absence"` pass after 72278555 (T26.7-4-6-3).
- T26.7-4-6-2: RT-37 is listed in the requirements of every error case and bound through the conformance suite; both execution modes compare each case with its expected.error.json (K277). Red: `make parity-declarations-check RULES=R13 IDS="RT-37"` exit 2 at f6196f47 (RT-37 cited by no unit); green: that check passes after b55da64c (T26.7-4-6-2).
- T26.7-4-6-1: RT-1 is cited by the unit runtime-operations, which binds the entry-point test of each implementation (ts, go, rust, php, php-ext with EntryPointsTest, python). Red: `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="runtime-operations"` fails at 8403c01c (the unit did not exist); green: it passes after 883e0fe8 (T26.7-4-6-1).
- T26.7-32-22: concurrent-bound-map cites only RT-70 and VAL-22, which its Go and Python tests check; the other ids it listed are unit requirements of T26.7-4-6-1 to -7. Red: `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="concurrent-bound-map" RULES=R15` exit 2 at 5d0d1ba5 (go and python cite RT-1 and more); green: `UNITS="concurrent-bound-map" RULES=R15` exit 0 after 06bd60e5 (T26.7-32-22).
- T26.7-2-8: the exceptions of the editor tests of language and of the CLI package begin with a kind that kit defines (ecosystem), so divergence-check holds. Red: `make divergence-check` exit 2 at 6479898d; green: divergence-check passes after 60a24060 (T26.7-2-8).
- T26.7-53-2: packages/php/tests/CliPackageTest.php is an internal test of php with a reason, since it checks the layout of the command line package and no requirement; R5 holds. Red: `make parity-declarations-check RULES=R5` exit 2 at 8491ec32; green: that check passes (T26.7-53-2).
- T26.7-32-25: conformance-cases runs the generated runner of the implementation that an implementation shares (shares; php-ext shares php), so php-ext needs no runner file of its own; the case tests/cases/data/null-assign passes in every language. Red: `make conformance-cases CASES=tests/cases/data/null-assign` exit 2 at 86294d6c (php-ext had no runner file); green: that run passes (T26.7-32-25).
- T26.7-32-20: closed; the row is [o] with its red at 36c51c61 and its green in the conformance cases (T26.7-32-20).
- T26.7-32-20: RT-44 states that an assign that is not a map fails with E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE and the message assign is not a map, as every implementation does; the conformance cases data/list-assign-error and data/number-assign-error hold it in every implementation. Red: `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="concurrent-bound-map" RULES=R15 IDS="RT-44"` exit 2 before the fix (56f37eedae96); green: that check and the conformance cases pass (T26.7-32-20).
- T26.7-53: the command line of the PHP library is a Composer project of its own, packages/cli/php (type project, K433), that requires the library and symfony/console; the library holds no command line and no console dependency. Red: `node node_modules/@polyspec/kit/dist/run-tests.mjs phpunit -- packages/php/tests/CliPackageTest.php` exit 1 at 2fb844d9 (the package did not exist); green: that test passes, `packages/cli/php/tests/CliTest.php` passes (7 tests of the command line), and the extension CLI test passes with the extension (T26.7-53).
- T26.7-4-5-1: closed on main: the red is at 4921fce5 and the green at 91845a9a and its test-only parts (T26.7-4-5-1).
- T26.7-4-5-1: the rules of docs/spec/editor.md that bind only the TypeScript editor tools (EDT-1 to EDT-7 and EDT-10 to EDT-21) move to docs/operations/editor-tools.md as prose without requirement ids; EDT-8 and EDT-9 stay, bound by the tokens and analysis units in every implementation; the units lexical-tables, editor-fixtures, editor-template-root and editor-documents go, and their tests are declared as internal entries of ts. Red: `make text-absent` on the moved ids at the parent holds the stale text; green: all pass, `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="tokens analysis"` and `make docs-check` pass (T26.7-4-5-1). Closed in the commit after 91845a9a (the red at 4921fce5 is recorded).
- T26.7-4-5-2: Python no longer exports `analyze_prefix`, which only the editor tools use; it is the private `_analyze_prefix`, out of `__all__` and the README tables, and its tests import the private name. Red: `make item-red` on the test commit: the three Python test files exit 1; green: test_exports, test_analysis and test_api pass, `make lint-python` and `make docs-check` pass (T26.7-4-5-2). Closed in the commit after ce136776 (T26.7-4-5-2).
- T26.7-67: the row is done; the wording fix of its changelog and checklist rows is in 70f2b5b9 and its state is recorded here (T26.7-67).
- T26.7-67: the T26.7-54 entry names the collect-all behavior of the validator without the fork in generic words, so the changelog holds no other project's behavior (T26.7-67).
- T26.7-32-19: VAL-13 states one neutral rule (each implementation converts host values by the binding table of its own language), the tables of TypeScript, PHP, Go, Rust and Python are text under it, and VAL-14, VAL-15, VAL-16 and VAL-23 are gone from the spec, the units and the comments; the new unit host-binding-tables binds each implementation’s table test (follows T26.7-32-18-1). Red: `make text-absent` on the four ids in data-model.md, data-model.ko.md and config/parity.json holds the stale text at be2aa831 (15 failures); green: all pass, and `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="host-binding-tables"` and `make docs-check` pass (T26.7-32-19).
- T26.7-64: the build of packages/ts prints its progress with the bracket prefix `[build-package]`, which kit reads as progress, so a build that does nothing is no finding of the commit gate. Red: `node node_modules/@polyspec/kit/dist/run-tests.mjs node -- tests/scripts/build-package.test.mts` exit 1 with the old prefix; green: 12 passed, 0 failed (T26.7-64). Red→green record: the old prefix made the test exit 1; with the kit prefix it passes 12 of 12 (T26.7-64).
- T26.7-32-18-1: RT-61 states the conversion of the typed generated program itself instead of citing RT-68, and the fixture form points to docs/operations/tools.md instead of RT-46; no spec file cites a moved id. Red: `make text-absent` on runtime.md and runtime.ko.md holds the stale "RT-46" and "RT-68"; green: all pass, and `make docs-check` passes (T26.7-32-18-1).
- T26.7-32-18: RT-38, RT-39, RT-43, RT-45 to RT-53, RT-54, RT-57, RT-58, RT-63, RT-64, RT-68 and RT-71 are tool rules of the build compiler, the browser package and the showcase, and leave docs/spec for docs/operations/tools.md; RT-56 stays. Red: `make text-absent` on each moved id at 91289159 holds the stale text (56 of 57 checks fail); green: they all pass on the branch, and `make docs-check` passes (T26.7-32-18).
- T26.7-51: the options of a program fail alike in every implementation, as its children T26.7-51-1 to -51-8 hold; its Verification is their owner tests and the parity of program-options and request-options. Red: `make item-red BEFORE=ee97996b` ran the Verification and it fails (`make test-php-ext CASES='EngineRefreshTest'` exits 2, `vitest run packages/ts/tests/render/engine.test.ts` exits 1, EngineRefreshTest 3 failed, `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="program-options request-options"` exits 2 with 19 findings); green: `make item-test ID=T26.7-51` passes every command (14 of 14, the Go pattern 2 passed) (T26.7-51).

- T26.7-32-6: EX-1 holds in every implementation: the case examples/page passes the conformance tests of all six, the PHP extension's since its Format classes are C. Red: `make test-php-ext CASES='ConformanceTest'` exit 2 at 7a415e9f (the extension loaded the library's Format class, "A source of the PHP library was loaded"); green: `make item-test ID=T26.7-32-6` runs the ten commands of the Verification cell, all pass (T26.7-32-6).

- T26.7-32-17: RT-3 is cited by the new unit render-targets; its test in every implementation renders the name a.tpl through the loader and the same source parsed as a template, with {x: 1}, and gets the same complete output (<p>1</p>); concurrent-bound-map no longer cites RT-3. Red: `make item-red` recorded `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="render-targets"` and `UNITS="concurrent-bound-map" RULES=R15 IDS="RT-3"` exit 2 before the fix. Green: they pass, the tests pass (ts 15 in engine.test.ts, go 1, rust 17, php 11, python 47), and `make parity-declarations-check` reports 2 findings fewer and none new. (T26.7-32-17).

- T26.7-32-16: VAL-18 is cited by the new unit native-object-arguments; its test in every implementation passes a native object to a host function directly, inside a list and inside a map, to an instance method and to a class function, and checks that each receives the original object; concurrent-bound-map no longer cites VAL-18. Red: `make item-red` recorded the parity-declarations-check UNITS="concurrent-bound-map" RULES=R15 IDS="VAL-18" command exit 2. Green: they pass, the tests pass (9 commands), and `make parity-declarations-check` reports 2 findings fewer and none new (T26.7-32-16).

- T26.7-62: docs/operations/dependencies.md names the Composer platform 8.4.1 that composerPlatforms of config/dependency-policy.json and the manifests declare, and the PHP 8.4 that CI tests. Red: `make item-red` recorded `grep -Fq 8.4.1 docs/operations/dependencies.md` exit 1 and `grep -Fq 8.4.1 docs/operations/dependencies.ko.md` exit 1 on main's text; green: `make docs-check` exit 0 and both greps pass (T26.7-62).

- T26.7-32-15: VAL-1 is cited by the conformance suite, whose case functions/type renders type() of each of the six value types and of a safe string, each under its own name and no other, so its test in every implementation checks VAL-1; concurrent-bound-map no longer cites VAL-1. Red: `make item-red` recorded `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="concurrent-bound-map" RULES=R15 IDS="VAL-1"` exit 2. Green: they pass, the tests pass (vitest 260, go test 261, cargo 260, PHPUnit and the PHP extension 780, Python 260), and `make parity-declarations-check` reports 2 findings fewer and none new. (T26.7-32-15).

- T22.6: the language-parity work of feat/parity-T22.6 is audited commit by commit: 6 changes are on main, 39 are superseded by main or kit, and the benchmark part (fa467fba, 2c37a634) is T22.6-17; the branch is deleted and the row waits for T22.6-17 (T22.6).

- T26.7-32-14: VAL-7 is cited by `host-function-rules`, whose test in every implementation registers a host function that returns `<b>` and renders it as `&lt;b&gt;`: the result is a plain string, never a safe one. The tests name VAL-7 beside FUN-43 to FUN-48. concurrent-bound-map no longer cites VAL-7. Red: `make item-red` recorded `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="concurrent-bound-map" RULES=R15 IDS="VAL-7"` exit 2. Green: it passes, the tests pass (vitest `engine.test.ts` 14, `go test` of TestHostFunctionRules 1, `cargo test --test functions` 16, PHPUnit and the PHP extension HostBindingTest 16, Python `test_object_calls.py` 13), the check of host-function-rules passes, and `make parity-declarations-check` reports 2 findings fewer and none new (T26.7-32-14).

- T26.7-32-13: RT-2 is cited by the conformance suite: the cases `include/not-found-error` and `block/undefined-block-error` name it, since they parse into an AST that holds `{+ missing.tpl}` and `{# nothing}` unresolved, the parse loading no other template, and fail only when they render, with `E_LOAD_NOT_FOUND` and `E_RUNTIME_BLOCK_UNDEFINED`. concurrent-bound-map no longer cites RT-2. Red: `make item-red` recorded `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="concurrent-bound-map" RULES=R15 IDS="RT-2"` exit 2. Green: it passes, the conformance suites pass (ts 260, go 261, rust 260, php and the PHP extension 780, python 260), the check of the conformance unit passes, and `make parity-declarations-check` reports 2 findings fewer and none new (T26.7-32-13).

- T26.7-32-12: VAL-19 names the error of each use of a native object that it forbids, which no rule named before: echoing it fails with `E_RUNTIME_STRINGIFY`, and a loop over it, a spread of it and `json` of a value that holds it fail with `E_RUNTIME_TYPE`; ERR-11 lists the native object under `E_RUNTIME_STRINGIFY`. The codes are those that every implementation already reports. The shared fixture of `tests/fixtures/native-object` gains `truthy.tpl` (a native object is truthy), `stringify.tpl`, `iterate.tpl` and `spread.tpl`, which the fixture tests of every implementation and `make generated-native-calls-check` run, and the new unit `native-object-opaque` cites VAL-19; the TypeScript titles of the fixture tests no longer name VAL-21, EXP-39 and EXP-38 for every entry. The error code lists of Rust, PHP and the PHP extension, generated from `errors.md`, and the arginfo hash of the stub follow the new text. concurrent-bound-map no longer cites VAL-19. Red: `make item-red` recorded, with the new fixture entries in place, the parity checks of the units (`native-object-opaque` unknown) and of VAL-19 on concurrent-bound-map exit 2. Green: both pass; the fixture tests pass (vitest 9, `go test` 1, `cargo test --test host_values` 1, PHPUnit and the PHP extension HostValuesTest 6, Python `test_object_calls.py` 13), the generated programs of the five languages match, the 34 test files of `packages/php-ext/tests` pass with the extension, `make error-codes-check` and `make lint-php-ext` pass, and `make parity-declarations-check` reports 2 findings fewer and none new (T26.7-32-12).

- T26.7-32-11: RT-33, RT-35 and RT-36 are cited by the new unit `render-limits`, whose test in every implementation reads the default limits through the public API (iterations 1,000,000, depth 32, outputBytes 16 MiB, expressionDepth 64), renders `ab{= 'cd'}` under an output limit of 4 bytes, and fails it under 3 bytes with `E_RUNTIME_LIMIT` at the echo that wrote past the limit (1:3), the failed render returning no output. Before, only Rust checked the defaults and only Python the output limit. The PHP extension binds the PHP test. concurrent-bound-map no longer cites the three ids. Red: `make item-red` recorded, with the new tests in place, `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="render-limits"` exit 2 and `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="concurrent-bound-map" RULES=R15 IDS="RT-33 RT-35 RT-36"` exit 2. Green: both pass, the tests pass (vitest `engine.test.ts` 14, `go test` 2, `cargo test --test functions` 16, PHPUnit and the PHP extension EngineRefreshTest 10, Python `test_api.py` 46), and `make parity-declarations-check` reports 6 findings fewer and none new (T26.7-32-11).

- T26.7-61: the php-ext tests write their anonymous classes as `new class ()`, as pint requires, so the tests pass the PHP format gate. Red: `make item-red ID=T26.7-61` recorded `make template-lint-php-format` exit 2 with pint failing FormatterClassTest.php, BindTest.php, RecordingProgram.php and PreparedRenderTest.php on new_with_parentheses and class_definition; green: `make template-lint-php-format` exits 0 (pint `passed`), `make build-php-ext` exits 0, the PHPUnit runs of FormatterClassTest 5, BindTest 7, PreparedRenderTest 4 and ProgramEngineTest 4 pass, and `make lint-php` passes (T26.7-61).

- T26.7-32-10: RT-40 states the default that it relies on: an engine whose options name no artifact refresh policy uses `true` (RT-76), and so parses a template once per `name@version`. Before, RT-40 said that of every engine, which the `dev` policy contradicts. The new unit `parse-reuse` cites RT-40, and its test in every implementation renders, with the default policy, a template from a loader whose source changes while its version stays, which renders the first parse, and then whose version changes, which renders the new source; every implementation already behaves so and defaults to `true`. The PHP extension binds the PHP test. concurrent-bound-map no longer cites RT-40. Red: `make item-red` recorded, with the new tests in place, `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="parse-reuse"` exit 2 (no such unit) and `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="concurrent-bound-map" RULES=R15 IDS="RT-40"` exit 2 (the R15 of RT-40 in go and python). Green: both pass, the tests pass (vitest `engine.test.ts` 13, `go test` 2, `cargo test --test functions` 15, PHPUnit and the PHP extension EngineRefreshTest 9, Python `test_loading.py` 9), and `make parity-declarations-check` reports 2 findings fewer and none new (T26.7-32-10).

- T22.6-17-1: the Python showcase adapter has the benchmark mode of the PHP adapter (`SHOWCASE_BENCH_ITERATIONS`, `SHOWCASE_BENCH_WARMUP`); a value that is not a non-negative integer fails and names its variable. Red: `make item-red ID=T22.6-17-1` (after `make typed-generator`) fails 7 of 8 tests of `tests/scripts/python-benchmark.test.mts` with no benchmark mode; green: `node node_modules/@polyspec/kit/dist/run-tests.mjs node -- tests/scripts/python-benchmark.test.mts` passes 8 of 8, and `make lint-python` and `make lint-ts` pass (T22.6-17-1).

- T26.7-32-8: the Verification also runs `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="concurrent-bound-map" RULES=R15 IDS="RT-7 RT-11 RT-23"` (kit K414-1), which reports the 6 R15 findings of the three ids on 3b5cdb5f, before they moved to the conformance suite, and none after; `make item-red BEFORE=3b5cdb5f` recorded that red, and with the other checks of its Verification the row is done (T26.7-32-8).

- T26.7-52-4: gofmt aligns the values of the table in the test of the load results without a source or a version that T26.7-52 added to `packages/go/load_failure_test.go`. Red: `make template-lint-go-format` exit 2, `gofmt -l` lists `packages/go/load_failure_test.go`; green: it passes, and TestLoadResultWithoutSourceOrVersionIsALoadFailure passes (T26.7-52-4).

- T26.7-51-8: rustfmt formats the test of the program options that T26.7-51-7 added to `packages/rust/tests/functions.rs` (the assert of the default limits wraps) and the import list of the file. Red: `make template-lint-rust-format` exit 2, diffs at `functions.rs` lines 5 and 231; green: it passes, and `cargo test --test functions` passes (T26.7-51-8).

- T26.7-32-9: RT-66 is cited by the new unit `page-cache-get-or-set`, whose test in every implementation checks the whole rule: a miss calls render once and returns its HTML, a hit returns the cached HTML without calling render, the entry is stored (`get` returns it), and it is stored with the TTL, since after 10 seconds the next `getOrSet` calls render again. The TypeScript, Go, Rust and PHP tests of a hit grow into this test, and Python, whose test only filled a missing key, gains it; the PHP extension binds the PHP test. concurrent-bound-map no longer cites RT-66. Red: `make item-red` recorded `make parity-declarations-check UNITS="page-cache-get-or-set"` exit 2 before the unit existed, with the new tests in place. Green: the check of the unit passes, the tests pass (vitest `page-cache.test.ts` 2, `go test` of TestPageCache 3, `cargo test --test page_cache` 2, PHPUnit and the PHP extension PageCacheTest 2, Python `test_api.py` 45), and `make parity-declarations-check` reports 2 findings fewer, the R15 of RT-66 on concurrent-bound-map in go and python, and none new (T26.7-32-9).

- T26.7-2-11: every child row is done (T26.7-2-11-1 and T26.7-2-11-2), so the row is done. Its Verification runs `make test-php-ext` on the library tests of its children (ParserTest, AnalyzeTest), their other owner tests with the extension of `var/php-ext`, and the parity check of the units bound to those tests (parse-spans delimiters delimiter-argument-error analysis error-object), in place of the unscoped check. Red: `make item-red BEFORE=ecb396d9` ran it on main before the first child, where `make test-php-ext` exits 2 because the tests load the Parser classes of the library; green: every command passes (T26.7-2-11).

- T26.7-2-7: every child row is done (T26.7-2-7-1 to T26.7-2-7-3), so the row is done. Its Verification runs `make test-php-ext` on the library tests of its children (FormatterTest, IndentTest, InvariantTest, ExpressionTest), their other owner tests with the extension of `var/php-ext`, and the parity check of the units bound to those tests (format-delimiters format-unchanged format-indentation format-raw-elements format-expression format-invariant), in place of the unscoped check. Red: `make item-red BEFORE=62b48de0` ran it on main before the first child, where `make test-php-ext` exits 2 because the tests load the Format classes of the library; green: every command passes (T26.7-2-7).

- T26.7-32-5: `make test-php-ext` runs the library tests of its units with the PHP extension and they pass (ConformanceTest, AnalyzeTest, FormatterTest), now that the extension registers the Formatter (T26.7-2-7-3); with the other checks of its Verification the row is done. Red: `make item-red BEFORE=7a415e9f` ran the Verification on main before T26.7-2-7-3, where `make test-php-ext` exits 2 because the tests load `packages/php/src/Format` of the library (T26.7-32-5).

- T26.7-51-5: the PHP extension checks the options of a program as the PHP library does (RT-4, RT-5, RT-6). `AstProgram::__construct` refuses a `limits` or `functions` that is not a map, `RuntimeEnvironment::__construct` reads the given limits in their order and refuses a key that RT-33 does not list (it ignored it) and a value that is not an integer, and refuses a function entry that is not callable with `functions.<name> is not a function` (it raised a TypeError), all with `InvalidArgumentException`; a `define` or an `env` that is a non-empty list is no map either, and a prepared render no longer adds `now` to such an env, which made a list look like a map. The library test of `RuntimeEnvironment` itself is new. Red: `make test-php-ext CASES='EngineRefreshTest'` failed 3 of 8 (a TypeError for `limits` 5, no error for `limits.loops`, `define 0: entry needs "template" or "html"` for a list define). Green: 8 of 8 with the extension and without it; HostBindingTest, LoadFailureTest, LoaderTest, BoundMapTest, ObjectCallsTest, ParserTest and CompilerInterfaceTest pass with the extension (49); lint-php-ext and lint-php pass (T26.7-51-5).

- T26.7-2-8-7: `BoundMap::bind`, `BoundMap::merge`, `Source::fromBytes`, `TemplateError::at` and `TemplateError::withoutPosition` of the PHP library name their class as their return type instead of `self`, as the PHP extension declares them (gen_stub takes no `self`), so `make test-php-ext` finds no difference between the declarations of the extension and of the library and runs its tests. Red: the new `ExportsTest::testTheFactoriesNameTheirClassAsTheirReturnType` fails on `BoundMap::bind`, and `make test-php-ext CASES='ExtensionClassesTest'` stops at the five `self` return types; green: ExportsTest passes with and without the extension, and `make test-php-ext` runs ExtensionClassesTest, 2 passed (T26.7-2-8-7).

- T26.7-2-8-6: the PHP extension's AstPreparedExecution has the public constructor of the library: an execution built from a program, the bound root data, the definitions, the environment, the target name and the loaded template renders that template. The constructor fills the state that prepare gives a prepared execution. Red: `run-tests.mjs phpunit --php-extension var/build/polyspec_template.so -- packages/php-ext/tests/PreparedExecutionTest.php` 0 passed, 2 failed, "AstPreparedExecution has no constructor"; green: the same command 2 passed (5 assertions), and AstProgramTest 8, ConformanceTest 768, ExprFixturesTest 73, ExportsTest 18, CompilerInterfaceTest 2 and RenderStateTest 8 passed; `make lint-php-ext` and `make lint-php` exit 0; `make parity-declarations-check` prints no line that names PreparedExecutionTest (the gate exits 2 on main for other rows) (T26.7-2-8-6).

- T26.7-4-6: `concurrent-bound-map` cites what its Go and Python tests check, RT-70 and VAL-22, and no longer the whole RT and VAL chapters; the conformance suite cites the 27 RT and VAL ids that its cases name (RT-4, RT-12 to RT-19, RT-21, RT-22, RT-24, RT-25, RT-27 to RT-32, RT-42, VAL-3 to VAL-6, VAL-8, VAL-11, VAL-20). The 47 RT and VAL ids that no case or bound test names stay on `concurrent-bound-map` until a unit binds the tests that check them, since no other unit cites them. The Go test of the concurrent renders names RT-70 in place of the stale RT-62a, and the Go test of a bound map names RT-62 where a prepared render renders twice. Red: `make parity-declarations-check` 310 findings on main, 179 of them the R15 of `concurrent-bound-map`. Green: 223 findings, none of them new; the Go and Python tests pass (go 259, python 259) and lint-go passes (T26.7-4-6).

- T26.7-2-6-3: the PHP extension registers `Render\RuntimeBindings` of the library as a final class, with the constructor and the methods of the library in its order: the operators, the members and calls, the entries, the spreads, the built-in and host calls and the limits are the C operations of the extension (the arity, the builtin table, the host calls, Helpers::toNumber, the bindings and the render state's Context), each failure a TemplateError at the node; `config/parity.json` names its php-ext symbol (api-runtime-bindings). Red: `run-tests.mjs phpunit --php-extension var/build/polyspec_template.so -- packages/php-ext/tests/RuntimeBindingsTest.php` 1 failed, "RuntimeBindings is not registered by the extension" (4 passed, 1 failed); green: the same command 5 passed, 0 failed; ExprFixturesTest, CompilerInterfaceTest and ConformanceTest of the Verification pass (73, 2 and 768 passed), and so do RenderStateTest, RegistryTest, ObjectCallsTest, HostBindingTest and ExportsTest (8, 2, 2, 16 and 18 passed); `make lint-php-ext` and `make lint-php` exit 0; `make parity-declarations-check` prints 0 findings for RuntimeBindingsTest (T26.7-2-6-3).

- T26.7-2-8-5: the PHP extension's AstProgram has the public `loadTemplate` of the library: the AST and the line index of a template that the loader gives (an AST that the loader gives is returned as it is), cached by version under the artifact refresh policy, and each loader failure located at the loading frame or at the name. The comments of `AstProgramTest.php` no longer name RT-9 (R16, the same finding that the internal test had). Red: `run-tests.mjs phpunit --php-extension var/build/polyspec_template.so -- packages/php-ext/tests/LoadTemplateTest.php` 4 errors, "Call to undefined method Polyspec\Template\AstProgram::loadTemplate()"; green: the same command 4 passed (7 assertions), and LoadFailureTest 5, EngineRefreshTest 5, ConformanceTest 768, ExprFixturesTest 73, ExportsTest 18, CompilerInterfaceTest 2 and RenderStateTest 8 passed; `make lint-php-ext` and `make lint-php` exit 0; `make parity-declarations-check` prints no line that names LoadTemplateTest or AstProgramTest (the gate exits 2 on main for other rows) (T26.7-2-8-5).

- T26.7-51-6: the Rust runtime adds the functions of the options as `register` adds them (RT-5, FUN-43, FUN-44), so `AstProgram::new` fails with an argument error for a name that is no identifier or a built-in, as the other languages do. `RuntimeEnvironment::new` now returns `Result<RuntimeEnvironment, ArgumentError>`, and the showcase adapter handles that result. Red: `run-tests.mjs cargo --cwd packages/rust -- --locked --test functions` did not compile, `error[E0599]: no method named err found for struct RuntimeEnvironment` and `error[E0599]: no method named is_ok found for struct RuntimeEnvironment`, because the test expected the new `Result`; before the change `RuntimeEnvironment::new` stored the map without the checks of `register`, so `AstProgram::new` accepted `"not a name"` and `length`. Green: the same command 12 passed, 0 failed; `run-tests.mjs cargo --cwd packages/rust -- --locked --test functions --test conformance --test object_calls --test compiler_interface` 276 passed, 0 failed; `make lint-rust` and `make template-lint-showcase-warnings` exit 0 The Rust programs that the checks of the generated code write (scripts/check-generated-*.mts, check-typed-generator.mts, check-package-installs.mts and tests/runner/conformance-generated-rust.mts) create their environment from an empty function map, which cannot fail, and take its result with `expect`; their Rust steps pass. (T26.7-51-6).

- T26.7-2-15-4: the extension registers Functions\Dates (parseOffset, daysFromCivil, civilFromDays, toUnixSeconds, format, table) of the library as a class, and its date and now table callables run the built-in functions of the extension with the environment they are given (an array of timezone and now; date reads the offset of the timezone first); the methods run the date operations of pt_functions.c, which are exposed rather than written again. Red: `run-tests.mjs phpunit --php-extension var/build/polyspec_template.so -- packages/php-ext/tests/DateGroupTest.php` 1 failed, "Polyspec\Template\Functions\Dates is not registered by the extension" (3 passed, 1 failed); green: the same command 4 passed, 0 failed; the FunctionsTest and ConformanceTest of the Verification pass (5 and 768 passed), and so do ExprFixturesTest, ExportsTest, CompilerInterfaceTest and RenderStateTest (73, 18, 2 and 8 passed); `make lint-php-ext` and `make lint-php` exit 0; `make parity-declarations-check` prints 0 findings for DateGroupTest (T26.7-2-15-4).

- T26.7-2-8-3: the PHP extension runs the command line of the library, `bin/template.php`, on the classes it registers under their names; the separate native command line (`bin/template-ext.php`, `Native\NativeRuntime`, `NativeAnalyzer`, `ExtensionNotLoaded`) and its `packages/php/src/Native` are removed. Red: `run-tests.mjs phpunit --php-extension var/build/polyspec_template.so -- packages/php-ext/tests/CliTest.php` 1 failure, `NativeRuntime still exists`; green: the same command 7 passed, 0 failed, and `run-tests.mjs phpunit -- packages/php/tests/CliTest.php` 6 passed, 0 failed (T26.7-2-8-3).

- T26.7-51-1: the spec states how the options of a program fail, which it did not say before (P33). RT-6: in TypeScript, PHP, the PHP extension and Python a `limits` that is not a map, a key that RT-33 does not list or a value that is not an integer fails the creation of the program with the argument error of ERR-13 (`limits is not an object`, `limits.<key> is not a limit`, `limits.<key> is not an integer`); in Go and Rust the `Limits` type holds only the limits of RT-33. RT-5: `functions` is a map of host functions, each added as `register` adds it, and a `functions` that is not a map or an entry that is not callable fails the creation the same way (`functions is not an object`, `functions.<name> is not a function`). RT-4: a `define` or an `env` that is not a map fails `prepare` and `render` of an `AstProgram` with `E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE`, `define is not an object` or `env is not an object`; TypeScript and the PHP extension do so today (Rust and Go take typed options, and the JSON readers of Rust use the same messages), while the PHP library and Python report them as `E_INTERNAL` or accept them. The implementations follow in the rows of T26.7-51 (T26.7-51-1).

- T26.7-51-2: TypeScript checks the `limits` and `functions` options when it creates a program (RT-5, RT-6): a `limits` or `functions` that is not a plain object, a `limits` key that RT-33 does not list, a limit that is not an integer and a `functions` entry that is not a function fail the creation with the argument error of ERR-13 and the message of the spec (`limits is not an object`, `limits.<key> is not a limit`, `limits.<key> is not an integer`, `functions is not an object`, `functions.<name> is not a function`). Red: `vitest run packages/ts/tests/render/engine.test.ts` 1 failed at `const error = failure(build);` with "no error was thrown" (11 passed, 1 failed); green: the same command 12 passed, 0 failed (T26.7-51-2).

- T26.7-51-3: the PHP library checks the options of a program as RT-4, RT-5 and RT-6 state (follows T26.7-51-1). A `limits` or a `functions` that is not a map, a limit key that RT-33 does not list, a limit that is not an integer, or a function that is not callable fails the creation of the `AstProgram` with `\InvalidArgumentException` and the messages `limits is not an object`, `functions is not an object`, `limits.<key> is not a limit`, `limits.<key> is not an integer` or `functions.<name> is not a function`. A `define` or an `env` that is not a map fails `prepare` and `render` with `E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE` and the message `define is not an object` or `env is not an object`, where a non-map define gave `E_INTERNAL`. Red: `run-tests.mjs phpunit --cwd packages/php -- --filter 'EngineRefreshTest'` has 2 failing tests: `testTheLimitsAndFunctionsOfAProgramAreChecked` with `TypeError: Polyspec\Template\Render\RuntimeEnvironment::__construct(): Argument #1 ($limits) must be of type array, int given`, and `testADefineOrAnEnvThatIsNotAMapIsUnsupported` with `Failed asserting that two strings are identical.` (expected `'E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE'`, actual `'E_INTERNAL'` for the define `'b.tpl'`, the first case). Green: the same command 7 passed, 0 failed; `HostBindingTest|LoadFailureTest|ConformanceTest` 789 passed, 0 failed; `make lint-php` exits 0 (T26.7-51-3).

- T26.7-51-4: Python checks the options of a program as the spec states (RT-4, RT-5, RT-6). A `limits` that is not a map, a key that is not a limit or a value that is not an integer (a bool is not one) fails the creation of the program with ValueError, as does a `functions` that is not a map or an entry that is not callable, with the messages of the spec; a `define` or an `env` that is not a map fails `prepare` and `render` with E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE, where an env was accepted before and a define gave E_INTERNAL. Red: `run-tests.mjs python --venv var/python/venv -- packages/python/tests/test_api.py` 42 passed, 12 failed; the first failure lines are `TypeError: 'int' object is not a mapping` for `limits=5` and `AssertionError: 'E_INTERNAL' != 'E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE'` for `define="b.tpl"`. Green: the same command 44 passed, 0 failed; `test_loading.py`, `test_object_calls.py` and `test_case_conformance.py` 277 passed, 0 failed; `make lint-python` exit 0 (T26.7-51-4).

Against main, `make parity-declarations-check` has no new finding and loses the 11 R2 missing-binding findings of php-ext; each bound library test passes with the extension, and so do the internal tests of php-ext (T26.7-2-8-2).

Its new case `block/body-whitespace-runs` renders `{#   card.tpl   title:t    no:n  }` (RT-29, RT-30) and formats it to `{# card.tpl title:t no:n}` with and without indentation, so the conformance, token and format tests of every implementation check that a block tag body keeps its words and replaces each run of whitespace with one space. `format-expression`, whose tests join expression tokens, no longer cites FMT-6. - T26.7-32-4: ERR-1 is cited by the new unit `error-object`, whose test in every implementation parses `a\n{? x}` as `x.tpl` and checks that the error has the fields `code`, `template`, `line`, `col`, `offset`, `end` and `message`, in that order (`E_PARSE_UNCLOSED_BLOCK`, `x.tpl`, 2, 1, 2, 3 and a message): a new test in ts, Go, Rust, PHP and Python, and the existing php-ext test, which now names ERR-1. `invalid-json`, whose tests compare only the code, no longer cites ERR-1. - T26.7-32-2: ERR-14 is cited by the new unit `bind-merge-errors`, whose tests of every implementation check the code, the empty template and the zero positions of a failed bind, so `invalid-json` no longer cites it. The Python tests gain `test_bind_fails_with_the_code_of_the_failed_check_without_a_position`, which checks `bind` of a value that is not a map, of a non-finite number, of a number out of range, of text that is not valid UTF-8 and of a list nested 65 deep, and `merge` of a value that is not a bound map, with E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE, E_DATA_NUMBER_NOT_FINITE, E_DATA_NUMBER_RANGE, E_DATA_INVALID_UTF8 and E_DATA_DEPTH. - T26.7-32-3: ERR-12 and ERR-13 are cited by the unit `internal-errors`, whose test in every implementation turns a panic or a language runtime error inside a render into `E_INTERNAL` for the template, and `invalid-json` no longer cites them. Go's check is its own test, `TestAPanicIsReportedAsAnInternalError`, moved out of `TestNativeObjectMembersAndArguments`; Python has `test_a_language_runtime_error_is_reported_as_an_internal_error`; the php-ext `HostBindingTest` has `testALanguageRuntimeErrorIsReportedAsAnInternalError`, a copy of the library test that was not run, because `test-php-ext` refuses on main until the extension build is fixed and T26.7-2-8 lands. - T26.7-32-1: AST-8, ERR-3, ERR-4 and EXP-39 are cited by units whose tests check them, so `parsed-ast`, `invalid-json` and `format-expression` no longer cite them. - T26.7-7-1: `tests/scripts/toolchain-files.test.mts` follows the tree again. - T26.7-2-2-2: the PHP extension registers `Polyspec\Template\Value\MapValue` as an internal final class with the signatures of the library: the insertion-ordered map over the PHP array of its private `items`, with `set`, `has`, `get`, `remove`, `count`, `keys`, `values`, `entries` and `copy`, numeric string keys behaving as PHP array keys and read back as strings. No internal method can return a `Generator`, so `MapValue::entries()` of the library and the extension returns the list of its `[key, value]` pairs, and its ten callers read it as `foreach (... as [$key, $value])`; `copy()` declares `MapValue`, which gen_stub requires in place of `self`. Red: the new `packages/php-ext/tests/MapValueTest.php` failed 4 of 4 (the class was the library's and `entries()` returned a Generator); green: under the extension `MapValueTest` 4, `Value/ValueTest` 7, `Functions/FunctionsTest` 5 and `ConformanceTest` 765 pass, `Value/ValueTest` 7 and `ConformanceTest` 765 pass with the library alone, and `make lint-php-ext` passes (T26.7-2-2-2).

- T26.7-2-1-5-1: `Engine` of the PHP extension hands a literal empty array given as target or options to its program without a fault. It rebuilt the arguments with `ZVAL_ARR`, which marks a zval as counted, so the immutable empty array of a literal `[]` got its count raised in read-only memory and the process ended with SIGBUS; it now passes the arguments of the caller as they are. Red: `ProgramEngineTest::testLiteralEmptyArraysReachTheProgram` did not finish, and `ConformanceTest` under the extension stopped at `block/anonymous-path-block` (510 passed, 1 failed), which T26.7-2-1-5 had not run; green: under the extension `ProgramEngineTest` 4 and `ConformanceTest` 765 pass and `make lint-php-ext` passes (T26.7-2-1-5-1).

Red: the new `packages/php-ext/tests/ValueClassesTest.php` failed 2 of 4 (`SafeString is not registered by the extension`, and `a list or map cannot be converted to text` for a safe string in the assign), while it passes 3 of 4 with the library alone (all but the registration); green: under the extension `ValueClassesTest` 4 and `Value/HostBindingTest` 14 pass, `Value/ValueTest`, `Functions/FunctionsTest` and `LoadFailureTest` pass, and `make lint-php-ext` passes (T26.7-2-2-1).

- T26.7-2-1-5: the PHP extension registers `Polyspec\Template\Engine` as an internal final class with the signatures of the library: a `Program` that holds a `Program` in the private readonly `program` and hands `prepare` and `render` to it with the target, the assign and the options unchanged (the defaults `[]` and `[]` when they are omitted), returning what it returns and passing what it throws. Red: the new `packages/php-ext/tests/ProgramEngineTest.php`, with its recording program `RecordingProgram.php`, failed 1 of 3 (`Engine is not registered by the extension`); green: under the extension `ProgramEngineTest` 3 and `EngineRefreshTest` 4 pass and `make lint-php-ext` passes; `CompilerInterfaceTest` now passes the assertions of `Engine` and stops at `AstProgram::$runtime` (T26.7-2-6) (T26.7-2-1-5).

- T26.7-2-1-4: the PHP extension registers `Polyspec\Template\AstProgram`, `PreparedExecution` and `AstPreparedExecution` as internal classes with the signatures of the library: the constructor with a loader object and the options `functions`, `limits`, `delimiters` and `artifact_refresh`, the readonly `loader`, `delimiters` and `artifactRefresh`, `parse`, `analyze`, `decodeTemplate` (the decode of the class `AstDecoder`, as in the library), `register`, `registerClass`, `hostFunction`, `classFunction`, `limits`, `prepare` and `render`; it renders a template that the loader object gives as a source or an AST, cached by its version under the refresh policy (RT-9, RT-40), and a template given as an AST array (RT-41), and binds a `Value\MapValue` of the library as a map, failing as `E_INTERNAL` when its keys and values cannot be read. A number literal of the AST arrays of the extension is a float, as the parser of the library makes it (AST-10), and the JSON text still writes `1e3` as `1000`. The extension reads an AST array and a loader result only as they are given: a literal value that is not of its kind, a missing name or a negative span is `E_INTERNAL`, a loader result without a version or without a source and an AST is `E_LOAD_FAILED`, and `functions` that are not an array are a `TypeError`. Red: with the extension built from the committed sources, the new `packages/php-ext/tests/AstProgramTest.php` failed 4 of 6 (`AstProgram is not registered by the extension`), and with the extension's AstProgram before the float change `AstDecodeTest::testADecodedTemplateIsTheAstThatParseReturnsAndRenders` failed (`1` against `1.0`); green: under the extension `AstProgramTest` 6, `BoundMapTest` of the extension 14 (its program now renders a bound map of the extension, RT-74), `EngineTest` 27, `AstDecodeTest` 5, `EngineRefreshTest` 4 and `ConformanceTest` 765 pass, and `make lint-php-ext` passes. Under the extension these stay red until their rows: `ExprFixturesTest` 56 and `CompilerInterfaceTest` 1 (`Render\Context` and `AstProgram::$runtime` need RuntimeServices, T26.7-2-6), `Value/BoundMapTest` 5 and `ExportsTest` 1 (a bound map of the library given to the extension, T26.7-2-2). `make duplication-check` reports the declarations of the stub as clones of the library; by S10 they are the declarations of the library, and kit K366 leaves the stub out of the check (T26.7-2-1-4).

## 0.0.11

- T24.38: release 0.0.11. Every manifest of `config/release.json` declares 0.0.11, and the locks, the dependency review, the compiled artifacts and the consumer projects of the release assets record it.

- T24.37: a generated PHP program renders a block of a template path whose argument has no root value, such as `{# part.tpl name:title}`, as the other targets do; it failed with `E_RUNTIME_TYPE` `generated input part.tpl.name is missing`, because it built the input before it read the argument from the scope of the block.

- T24.36: a generated TypeScript, PHP or Python program fails on a `define` option that is not an object with the argument error `define is not an object` (ERR-13). TypeScript raised a `BindError` with `E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE`, PHP an `E_INTERNAL` error of the type of its parameter and Python an `AttributeError`.

- T24.35: template pins `@polyspec/kit` 0.0.52, and `make kit-sync` wrote its copies (`release.yml`, which runs kit-check before any release step, `kit-bootstrap.mk`, `config/layout.kit.yml`). `make lint-ts` gives no configuration hint about the copy of knip.json any more, and npm allows the remote kit package in every recipe.

## 0.0.10

- T24.34: release 0.0.10. Every manifest of `config/release.json` declares 0.0.10, and the locks, the dependency review, the compiled artifacts and the consumer projects of the release assets record it.

- T24.33: a request that does not match the declared types of a generated program fails with the same argument error text in every target (ERR-13): `<path> is required` or `<path> is not <expected>`, with the path of the value in the request, such as `assign.page.name is not a string`, `assign.tags[1] is not a string` or `define.card.data.title is not a string`. Rust wrote `title: expected a string`, PHP and Python `has an invalid type`, and Go the error of encoding/json; Go and Python accepted a missing required root field, and Go reported definition data that is not an object as `E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE` instead of the argument error `define.<id>.data is not an object`. `make generated-arguments-check` renders the requests of `messages.json` of `tests/fixtures/typed-arguments` and `typed-arguments-unread` in every target and compares the exact results.

- T24.32: `@polyspec/template/decode` exports `decodeTemplate` and `AstDecodeError` alone, and a browser bundle that imports only `decodeTemplate`, from that entry or from `@polyspec/template/render`, is 86,538 bytes minified and 7,490 gzip instead of 127,057 and 13,479. The validator of the AST schema calls one function for each definition of the schema instead of a copy of its code at each `$ref` (ajv `inlineRefs: false`), with every check and message kept, and the decode entry has a chunk without the runtime that the other entries share.

- T24.31: a generated Rust program declares only the records that its types name, the fields of `Assign` that a template reads, the template and data of a definition only when a block reads them, and `to_value` only when a value is converted back, so each showcase program passes clippy `-D warnings` as a private module of a binary crate. A root field or definition data that the program does not read is checked against its declared type without a value of that type, and fails with the same field path, such as `page: name: expected a string` or `define.card.data.name: expected a string`.

## 0.0.9

- T24.30: release 0.0.9. Every manifest of `config/release.json` declares 0.0.9, and the locks, the dependency review, the compiled artifacts and the consumer projects of the release assets record it.

- T24.29: a generated Rust or TypeScript program declares only what it uses, so a consumer that includes the Rust program as a private module of a binary crate passes clippy `-D warnings` and the TypeScript program passes oxlint `no-unused-vars`. Rust declares `Definition<T>` only when the program declares a definition, `DefinitionData<T>` only for a template that a definition names, no `ArtifactManifest` and no `render_values` for a static root, which only returned an error; without definitions `generated_bind_definitions` fails on the first entry of the request. TypeScript reads `raw` of a definition entry only when a definition is declared and declares the size of a loop only when the loop reads it, its last item or has an empty branch.

## 0.0.8

- T24.28: release 0.0.8, which supersedes 0.0.7. The tag v0.0.7 has no published release: the proof of the release workflow ran the Composer consumer with the PHP 8.3.6 of the runner, which the platform check of Composer refused. Every manifest of `config/release.json` declares 0.0.8, and the locks, the dependency review, the compiled artifacts and the consumer projects of the release assets record it.

- T24.27: `.php-version` declares PHP 8.5, the minimum release of `config/toolchain.json`; the release workflow of kit starts setup-php only with that file, and every setup-php step of `ci.yml` and `dependency-review.yml` reads it.

- T24.26: the wheel `polyspec-template` requires `jsonschema==4.26.0`, the version that `packages/python/pylock.toml` pins, and that lock is the lock of its consumer, so `make release-assets` accepts the wheel.

## 0.0.7

- T24.25: release 0.0.7. Every manifest of `config/release.json` declares 0.0.7, and the locks, the dependency review, the compiled artifacts and the consumer projects of the release assets record it.

- T24.22: template pins `@polyspec/kit` 0.0.50, and `make kit-sync` wrote its copies (`knip.json`, `kit-bootstrap.mk`). The php bindings of parity run the PHPUnit of the root `vendor/`, which kit 0.0.48 reported as not executed. `config/knip-entries.json` declares make, php and composer, which the scripts and tests run, in `binaries`. `packages/php-ext/composer.json` has its own entry in `composerPlatforms` of `config/dependency-policy.json` and a `composer.lock`, so `composer validate --strict` reads it; Composer loads no package of the type `php-ext` through the path repository of the root.

- T25.8: a generated TypeScript or Go program decodes a value once, where it enters from the runtime, the render scope or the request, and never on a read. A local is a variable of the generated function that holds its typed value, and every read uses it without a check; before, each read of a local decoded its whole value from the render scope, by a decoder in TypeScript and a JSON round trip in Go. The scope keeps the Value of each local for includes and dynamic lookups, and after an include the program decodes once each local that the included templates may assign (RT-21). A loop iterates a typed list or map as it is instead of encoding it and decoding each item. An error of an intermediate result in TypeScript names its expression in the syntax of the template, such as `label(page.title) is not a string`, instead of `index`, `+` or the name of the function. The output of every program is unchanged; the committed artifacts, manifests and `examples/site/index.html` record the new compiler digests.

- T24.20: template pins `@polyspec/kit` 0.0.48, and `make kit-sync` wrote its copies (`release.yml`, the bootstrap scripts, `config/layout.kit.yml`). Each checkout runs the Node.js 26.12.0 of `mise.toml`, which `make install-tools TOOLS=npm` installs with mise into `var/tools`; `.node-version` is gone, `engines.node` of every package is `>=26.12.0`, and every recipe starts node as `$(NODE)` and go as `$(GO)`, since make 3.81 ran the node of the machine for a bare `node`. The workflows pin actions/checkout v7.0.1, actions/setup-node v7.1.0 and shivammathur/setup-php 2.40.0 by SHA, and the job ci-passed installs the bootstrap and the packages before `make ci-passed`, which had failed in every run because kit.mk was missing.

- T25.6-1: every runtime decodes the AST JSON by one rule (AST-10): it validates the JSON against `contracts/schema/ast.schema.json` with the JSON Schema validator of its ecosystem, and an invalid AST fails with the JSON Pointer of the invalid node, the deepest instance location of the errors, the least in code point order at that depth. The schema selects the definition of a statement node, an expression node, a list item, a map entry and the value type of a literal by `if`/`then` instead of `oneOf`, and a nullable field by `if`/`else`; it accepts the same documents. Rust validates with the crate jsonschema before serde, so it now checks the identifier pattern, the kind of a literal value, `minLength` and `minItems`, and `AstDecodeError` is an enum of `Json` and `Invalid` whose `path()` is the pointer; Go's `ast.DecodeTemplate` validates with santhosh-tekuri/jsonschema/v6 and fails with an `*ast.DecodeError` that names the pointer, so it no longer accepts unknown fields; PHP publishes `AstProgram::decodeTemplate`, validated with opis/json-schema, which throws an `AstDecodeError`, and Python `decode_template`, validated with jsonschema, which raises an `AstDecodeError` and returns spans as tuples and number literals as floats, as `parse` makes them. TypeScript names the same pointer, so an unknown node type is `/body/0/type` instead of `/body/0`. Go, Rust, Python and PHP hold a copy of the schema that a test compares with the contract file. Python depends on jsonschema, pinned with its dependencies by `packages/python/pylock.toml`, which `make python-lock` writes, `make python-lock-check` checks under `make dependency-audit`, `make python-package` installs and the Python install check installs from the uv cache; `make install` makes the environment of the Python package. The showcase adapter of the Go workspace no longer requires the workspace module at v0.0.0, which Go then looked up on the proxy, and `go.work.sum` holds the checksums of the workspace. The showcase adapters of TypeScript, JavaScript, Rust, PHP and Python decode the compiled AST with their runtime. The parity unit `parsed-ast` binds the decode test of every runtime, and the unit `ast-decode-errors` the test of the invalid ASTs of `tests/fixtures/ast-decode/invalid.json`, which every runtime reads.

- T25.5: the generated TypeScript states its types without assertions, and kit's type-aware oxlint reports nothing in it: a value of a type of the type manifest has its TypeScript type (an optional value is `T | null`, any is `Value`), a value that holds a record becomes a Value through the encoder of its record, and a Value that the program reads back from the scope or the runtime is checked by a decoder, which fails with `E_RUNTIME_TYPE` at the position of the value while the program renders. A block builds its input field by field and fails with `E_RUNTIME_TYPE` when a required field has no value, as in PHP. `RenderOptions.define` and `env` of `@polyspec/template` are `unknown`, and the engine and the generated program fail with `define is not an object` or `env is not an object` on a value that is no plain object, such as a `Map` or a list. `make typed-generator-compile-check` type checks the generated programs with the strict options of the repository.

- T24.17: the PHP side of the native engine is in `polyspec/template`: `Polyspec\Template\Native\NativeRuntime`, `Polyspec\Template\Native\NativeAnalyzer` and the command `bin/template-ext.php`, which Composer installs as `vendor/bin/template-ext.php`. Without the extension `polyspec_template` they throw `Polyspec\Template\Native\ExtensionNotLoaded`, whose message names `pie install polyspec/template-php-ext`, and the command exits with status 1. `polyspec/template-php-ext` is the C extension only, as PIE installs it: no `php/`, no `bin/` and no `require` of `polyspec/template`; before, its classes and command shipped in a package that neither Composer nor PIE installs into a project. The Composer smoke command of the release consumer runs `vendor/bin/template.php parse` and expects the error of `vendor/bin/template-ext.php` without the extension.

The compiler validates the AST artifact manifest, the type manifest, the generated manifest and the function contract with ajv, and reads each AST with `decodeTemplate` of `@polyspec/template`, so an invalid document fails with the errors of its schema; `ajv` is a dependency of the package and the unused `recordsExtra` of the type manifest is gone. `make test-compiler-types` builds the package and type checks it. The generated programs are unchanged byte for byte; the committed manifests and `examples/site/index.html` record the new compiler digests.

The hand-written TypeScript reads JSON through schemas and narrows unknown values by checks instead of casts, `any` and non-null assertions. A runtime fix came with it in `@polyspec/template`: a hole of a sparse host array binds as null instead of undefined. `PrefixAnalysis` is a union whose AST is present exactly when it has no error.

FUN-37 states the range of an offset, and the cases `functions/date-offset-hour-error` and `functions/date-offset-minute-error` check it in every runtime.

The order `parse FILE [options]` is no longer accepted.
