RED: `make rules-check` reported `uncovered: CNF-16 EDT-18 EDT-19 EDT-20 EDT-21`. GREEN: it reports 0 uncovered.

- T23.2: Go, PHP and the PHP extension decide the assignment form of a `{:` tag as TypeScript does (LEX-6). Go regexp has no lookahead, so the Go parser rejects an operator `=` that `=` or `>` follows after the match of `assignHead`, where it took `{:x == 1}` and `{:x => 1}` as assignments; Go, PHP and the PHP extension matched the head on the first 80 bytes of the tag body only, so a name longer than about 78 bytes made an assignment the else tag. Each now matches the head on the rest of the text (PHP with `\G` at the offset). Rust and Python already decided as TypeScript. The conformance cases `errors/assign-head-equality`, `errors/assign-head-arrow` and `errors/assign-head-long-name` hold every implementation to the error of TypeScript, and `TestAssignmentHeadDecidesAsTypeScript` of the Go parser compares 10 inputs with the code and offsets of TypeScript. RED: the Go test failed 9 of 10 inputs, and the cases failed in Go (3 of 3), PHP and the PHP extension (the long name). GREEN: the Go test passes, the error cases pass 108 of 108 in six implementations, and `make conformance` passes 1554 of 1554.

- T24.12: template pins `@polyspec/kit` 0.0.42. Go, Python, uv, ruff, vulture, alint, golangci-lint, deadcode, govulncheck, cargo-audit and Composer are tools of `mise.toml`, which `make toolchain-lock` locks in `mise.lock` for linux-x64, linux-arm64, macos-arm64 and macos-x64 and `make install-tools` installs with mise; `config/toolchain.json` keeps php, pythonBackend, the exceptions and the release of mise, and `.python-version` is deleted. The recipes and `scripts/python-toolchain.mjs` run Python by the shim `var/tools/mise/shims/python3`, `make install-tools` takes no tool names, and setup-php installs no Composer. `config.platform.php` of the two Composer packages is `8.5.0`, the platform of `config/dependency-policy.json`, the sprintf-js advisory that kit brings is accepted as kit accepts it, and `make dependency-review RECORD=1` wrote the review record. RED: `make test-vscode-integration` and `tests/scripts/install-vscode.test.mjs` failed with `ERR_PACKAGE_PATH_NOT_EXPORTED` for `@polyspec/kit/install-vscode`, `make dependency-policy-check` crashed on the workspace pattern `packages/template-ts`, and `make dependency-review RECORD=1` failed in install-tools because uv reported two Python 3.14.8 installations.

- T24.3: `make runtime-interface-check` and `make compiler-interface-check` parse the TypeScript sources and the compiler backends with oxc-parser instead of the JavaScript API of the TypeScript compiler, which TypeScript 7 does not have; `docs/spec/compiler.md` and `docs/spec/runtime.md` name it. The mutation check had passed while every mutation crashed the check. RED: both checks failed with `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'Latest')` at `ts.ScriptTarget.Latest`. GREEN: both pass, the TypeScript, Go, Rust, PHP and Python declarations pass, and the 22 structural mutations are rejected each by the finding of its mutation.

- T23.5: the Rust filesystem loader identifies a file by a `same_file::Handle` of the crate `same-file` (device and inode number on Unix, volume serial number and file index on Windows) instead of `std::os::unix::fs::MetadataExt`, so the crate no longer builds only on Unix; it reads the file through the handle and keeps the handle of each file it read. RT-10 names the Windows identity. RED: `rustc --print cfg --target x86_64-pc-windows-msvc` has no `unix`, and `std::os` declares `unix` only for `target_family = "unix"`, so `use std::os::unix::fs::MetadataExt` does not resolve on Windows (the Windows standard library cannot be installed offline). GREEN: the crate uses no Unix-only API, `filesystem/*` passes 8 of 8 in Rust, and `make test-rust` passes 49 of 49.

- T23.5: the editor resolves include paths against a declared root. `template-root.json` (`{"schema": 1}`) in the nearest ancestor directory of a document declares its root (EDT-18); an invalid marker is an `E_ROOT_INVALID` diagnostic on the marker (EDT-19), and the LSP server publishes it on the URI of the marker. For a text that parses, the language service reports every include path and every block path with a path token that leaves the root (`E_LOAD_OUTSIDE_ROOT`), crosses a symbolic link (`E_LOAD_FAILED`) or names no regular file (`E_LOAD_NOT_FOUND`), and every such path as `no template root declared (template-root.json)` (`E_ROOT_UNDECLARED`) without a valid marker (EDT-20). `openDocument` reaches files only through `options.location` with an injected host (EDT-21): the LSP server passes a host over `node:fs` for `file:` URIs, and the CodeMirror adapter takes `options.location`. `analyzePrefix` of `@polyspec/template` returns the AST of a source that parses, and `PathError` is exported. The editor fixtures lie under a marker, with the new fixture `paths.tpl`. RED: `tests/root.test.ts` of the language service failed 18 of 19 cases, the LSP tests 5 of 21 and the CodeMirror tests 4 of 61. GREEN: `make test-language` passes 729 tests, `make test-lsp` 21 and `make test-codemirror` 61 with its browser test.

- T23.5: the filesystem loader of every implementation (TypeScript, Go, Rust, PHP, the PHP extension and Python) examines each component of a name below the root with `lstat` and never follows a symbolic link: a link at the file or at a directory fails with `E_LOAD_FAILED`. It gives one file one name: a name whose file, by device and inode number, the loader already read under another name fails with `E_LOAD_FAILED`. RT-10 states both rules. `tests/fixtures/filesystem/cases.json` holds 8 filesystem cases with symbolic and hard links, which `tests/runner/conformance.mjs` writes into a temporary directory and runs in every implementation as `filesystem/<name>` (CNF-16). RED: the cases passed 2 of 8 in every implementation: the links were followed, and a hard link of the entry failed with `E_LOAD_CYCLE`. GREEN: 8 of 8 pass in all six implementations.

- T23.3: the VS Code extension has no template grammar. Its TextMate grammar `text.html.polyspec-template` only includes `text.html.basic`, which embeds CSS and JavaScript; the tag injection with its repository is deleted, and the template tags are colored by the semantic tokens of the server, which the extension already maps. A regular expression grammar has no parser context and disagreed with `analyze`. The extension contributes `editor.semanticHighlighting.enabled` `true` for the language as a configuration default. The grammar tests of `vscode-tmgrammar-test` (`tests/grammar/*.tpl`, `make test-vscode-grammar`) are deleted with the packages `vscode-tmgrammar-test` and `tm-grammars`; the integration check of `_workbench.captureSyntaxTokens` is replaced by semantic token assertions for a tag in text and a tag inside an attribute value. EDT-15 and `docs/operations/editor-tools.md` state it. RED: `make test-vscode-unit` failed in the cases of the grammar (it held the injection) and of the configuration defaults (no `editor.semanticHighlighting.enabled`), and `make test-vscode-integration` failed `semantic highlighting is enabled for the language` with `configuredByTheme`. GREEN: `make test-vscode-unit` passes 12 of 12, `make test-vscode-types` passes, and `make test-vscode-integration` passes 17 of 17 and 2 of 2 checks.

- T24.3: the VS Code extension declares `engines.node` `>=24.18.1`, the Node.js of the Electron of VS Code 1.138.0, its minimum `engines.vscode`, on which VS Code runs the extension and its server; the pin of kit, 26.11.1, is false for that runtime. An exception of `config/toolchain.json` with the scope `packages/template-vscode` and the channel 24.18.1 lets the toolchain check accept it. RED: `make test-vscode-unit` failed in `manifest supports untrusted and virtual workspaces and declares no Node.js engine` with the engines `node` and `vscode`. GREEN: the case, now named for the Electron Node.js, and the 11 other cases pass, and `make toolchain-check TOOLS=node` reports no floor or exception finding.

- T23.1: the parser is the only grammar. `analyze` and `analyzePrefix` of `@polyspec/template` return the tokens of every part of each accepted tag, classified by EDT-8 (delimiters, sigils, loop and assignment heads, paths, block words, the directive), and each tag carries `bodyStart` and `bodyEnd`; `analyzePrefix` returns the tokens of the accepted tags only. `@polyspec/template-language` deletes the lexical tables it kept beside the parser (`SIGILS`, `OPERATOR`, `WRAPPERS`, `LOOP_HEAD`, `ASSIGN_HEAD`, `RESERVED`, `IDENT` and the delimiter-directive regex) and its `tags.ts`; `tests/lexical-tables.test.ts` fails on a regular expression or a list of strings of the language service that it does not list with its reason.

- T24.3: the TypeScript packages emit their declarations with the TypeScript 7 compiler. The declaration build of tsup (`dts`) calls the JavaScript API of the compiler, which TypeScript 7 does not have, so `scripts/build-package.mjs` runs tsup without `dts` and then `tsc --emitDeclarationOnly` with `tsconfig.json` and `tsconfig.node.json` for `template-ts` and a new `tsconfig.build.json` (the sources only) for `template-language`, `template-lsp` and `template-codemirror`. The declarations follow the directories of the sources, so the build publishes the files of subdirectories, the `types` of `@polyspec/template/node` is `dist/node/index.d.ts`, and `template-codemirror` packs `dist/**/*.d.ts`. RED: `make build-ts` failed on a fresh install with `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'useCaseSensitiveFileNames')`. GREEN: `make build-vscode` and `make build-codemirror` build, the type checks of the five packages pass, and `node --test tests/scripts/build-package.test.mjs` passes its 12 cases, whose reader now follows the declarations in subdirectories.

- T24.9: `config/owner-checks.json` owns `tests/scripts/driver-fixture.mjs` with the tests that copy the drivers with it, `tests/scripts/drivers.test.mjs`, `tests/scripts/failure-messages.test.mjs` and `tests/scripts/no-silent-skips.test.mjs`. RED: `make owner-validate` failed with `tests/scripts/driver-fixture.mjs: the path matches no owner in config/owner-checks.json`. GREEN: it reports that the configuration owns every tracked path.

- T24.7: `config/parity.json` declares the six implementations of template (TypeScript, Go, Rust, PHP, the PHP extension and Python), the profile `runtime`, the shared suites (the conformance cases of `tests/cases` and the expression vectors of `tests/fixtures/expr/cases.json`) with the native test of each implementation that binds them, the comparators and the single-language tools, and the exclusions of the Python conformance binding and the PHP extension expression binding with their task T24.8. `make parity-declarations-check` and `make parity-check` of kit replace the feature registry of template: `contracts/features.json`, `scripts/features/`, `make feature-check`, `scripts/check-language-test-matrix.mjs` with its mutation check and `make language-test-matrix`, `tests/runner/parity.mjs` with `make parity`, and `tests/scripts/feature-python-coverage.test.mjs` are deleted. CNF-11 states the parity of the shared suites, and `docs/features.md` keeps the status table without a generator. `make parity-declarations-check` reports 67 findings, which T24.8 removes.

- T23.4: `config/editor-boundaries.json` forbids the subpaths of the modules that the deleted checker forbade by prefix: `node:*/**` and `vscode-*/**` join `node:*` and `vscode-*`, since a picomatch `*` and a trailing `**` after `node:` match one segment only. RED: `import 'vscode-languageserver/node'` and `import 'node:fs/promises'` in `packages/template-language/src` passed the check. GREEN: the check reports both imports, and the repository passes with `4 packages of 2 boundaries passed, 29 files checked`.

- T23.4: the editor boundary checker and the VS Code installer come from kit. `make editor-boundary-check` runs the checker of kit on `config/editor-boundaries.json`, which declares the rules of EDT-2 (the adapters `template-lsp`, `template-codemirror` and `template-vscode` neither declare nor import `@polyspec/template`) and EDT-3 (the language service uses no Node.js, editor or CodeMirror module and no DOM global outside `src/cli`); `make install-vscode` runs the installer of kit for `packages/template-vscode`, and the integration run reads the copy with `installedExecutable` of `@polyspec/kit/install-vscode`. `scripts/check-editor-boundaries.mjs` and `scripts/install-vscode.mjs` are deleted, and `tests/scripts/install-vscode.test.mjs` keeps the case of the integration run. RED: `node --test tests/scripts/install-vscode.test.mjs` failed with `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` after the deletion, and `make editor-boundary-check` failed without `config/editor-boundaries.json`. GREEN: the test passes, and the check reports `4 packages of 2 boundaries passed, 29 files checked`.

- T24.5: the Python is linted with the root `ruff.toml` that kit copies only: `[tool.ruff]` of `packages/template-python/pyproject.toml` is deleted, since an editor or a plain ruff run applies the nearest configuration. `config/toolchain.json` declares alint, which `make layout-check` runs. RED: `make layout-check` reported `packages/template-python/pyproject.toml: error: lint-ruff-nested`. GREEN: it reports no ruff finding.

- T24.4: the showcase Go adapter resolves the Go package of template through the root `go.work`, which uses `packages/template-go` and `tools/showcase/adapters/go`, instead of a `replace` of its `go.mod`; `docs/operations/publication.md` shows a consumer the same through its own `go.work`. RED: `make lint-go` failed with `tools/showcase/adapters/go/go.mod:7:1: local replacement are not allowed` (gomoddirectives of `.golangci.yml`). GREEN: gomoddirectives reports nothing, and the adapter builds and `go vet ./...` of `packages/template-go` passes in workspace mode.

- T24.3: the toolchain floors are the pins of kit. `.node-version` is 26.11.1 and `engines.node` of every `package.json` is `>=26.11.1`; `require.php` of every `composer.json` is `>=8.5` and `config.platform.php` is `8.5`, with the content hashes of the Composer locks that Composer computes, and the suite and CI run PHP 8.5 only (`config/toolchain.json` `php`, the dependency policy); `requires-python` stays `>=3.14`; the `go` line of both `go.mod` files is 1.27.2 with no toolchain line; `rust-toolchain.toml` and `rust-version` of both Cargo packages are 1.99.0; `typescript` is 7.0.2, and the dependency exception that kept TypeScript 6 for the lint peer range of typescript-eslint is deleted with it. RED: `make toolchain-check` failed with 21 manifest floors and 2 declarations that differ from the pins of kit. GREEN: it reports no floor or declaration, and `node --test tests/scripts/toolchain-files.test.mjs`, whose Node.js case now expects `engines.node` `>=` `.node-version`, passes.

- T24.6: the TypeScript is linted by `make lint-ts` of kit, oxlint with oxlint-tsgolint (`.oxlintrc.json`) and knip (`knip.json`), instead of eslint with typescript-eslint, which supports no TypeScript 7; `eslint.config.mjs`, `make lint-js` and the packages `eslint`, `@eslint/js` and `typescript-eslint` are deleted. The lint targets of kit `lint-go`, `lint-rust` and `lint-php` replace the targets of template of the same names, which become the format checks `lint-go-format` (gofmt), `lint-rust-format` (`cargo fmt --check`) and `lint-php-format` (Pint); `config/toolchain.json` declares vulture, golangci-lint and deadcode, which the lint targets of kit run, and the sha256 of the Composer phar, with which `make install-tools` installs phpcs and PHPStan of the PHP lint. The full suite and the CI jobs run the new targets, and `config/owner-checks.json` names them. `make lint-ts` runs; its findings are a task of this wave.

- T24.2: npm writes its links. `.npmrc` with `install-links=true` and `bin-links=false` is deleted, and so are the workarounds that it forced: the root `package.json` declares the JavaScript packages as npm workspaces instead of `file:` dependencies with overrides, `scripts/build-package.mjs` reads the builds of the workspace packages it depends on through their links and no longer installs a built package again (`--install` is gone), the recipes, `scripts/tools.mjs` and `make release-check` start a tool from `node_modules/.bin`, and `tests/scripts/no-symlinks.test.mjs` is deleted. `make release-check` installs its checkout with `make install-tools TOOLS=npm`, `make install-packages` and `make install`. RED: `make toolchain-check` failed with `.npmrc: install-links is true` and `.npmrc: bin-links is false`, and `node --test tests/scripts/build-package.test.mjs` failed its prerequisite builds, because the copy of `@polyspec/template` held no build. GREEN: both pass, with `tests/scripts/atomic-publish.test.mjs` and `tests/scripts/clean-release.test.mjs`.

- T24.1: the shared tools come from the npm package `@polyspec/kit` instead of vendored copies. The Makefile includes `scripts/kit/kit-bootstrap.mk`, which includes the `kit.mk` of the installed package, and the recipes run its tools as `node $(KIT)/<tool>.$(KIT_EXT)`; `scripts/kit/*`, `tests/kit/*`, `.kit/kit.lock.json`, `kit.json` and the target `kit-test` are deleted. The files of `kit.copies` are copies that `make kit-sync` writes: the bootstrap of `scripts/kit/`, `.github/workflows/release.yml`, `PRINCIPLES.md`, `config/layout.kit.yml`, the lint configurations, the schemas of `config/` and `.editorconfig`, whose folders of byte-exact or generated files hold a `.editorconfig` of their own; `config/layout.yml` holds the layout map of template. `make install-tools TOOLS=npm` and `make install-packages` install npm and the packages, and `make install` installs Go, uv, Python, the Rust toolchain, the crates (`cargo-downloads-fetch`), the Composer packages and the Python build requirements; no CI job sets up Go, since kit installs the Go of its pin. The pre-push hook runs `make push-gate-hook`. `config/toolchain.json` declares uv, which installs the Python of `pythonBuild`; `config/dependency-policy.json`, `config/dependency-review.json` and `config/release.json` take the shapes of the kit schemas, with the wheel lock `packages/template-python/pylock.wheel.toml` that uv writes. The tests of the recipes and workflows expect the tools of the package, read the nested `.editorconfig` files with picomatch and leave the copies of kit to `make kit-check`. RED: `node --test tests/scripts/test-targets.test.mjs tests/scripts/editorconfig.test.mjs tests/scripts/suite-parity.test.mjs` failed 16 of 21 cases on the vendored tree. GREEN: they pass with the package installed.

- T22.5-11: `stopProcessGroup` in `scripts/process-group.mjs` sends its signal to the group on every probe while the group answers, not once. A member forked while the first SIGKILL was delivered survived it, and the stop then waited forever for the group to end, which made `a stopped process group writes nothing into a directory that is then removed` time out at 300 s. A process group id is not reused while a member exists, so the repeated signal reaches only the group of the stop; the one window is between a successful probe and the next signal. While it waits longer than a second, the stop lists the members of the group that `ps` shows.

- T22.5-10: the leader of `tests/scripts/process-group-callers.test.mjs` and `tests/scripts/process-group-exit-window.test.mjs` writes the pid of the member it spawns into the pid file before it continues, in place of the member writing its own pid after it starts. The member starts running after `MEMBER_START_MS` (5 s) in both tests, so the old design failed with ENOENT before the assertions. The case after its deadline starts `/bin/sh` as its leader, which writes the member pid at fork time, so the file exists before the deadline. Its deadline is 10 s, about 100 times the time the leader needs before the file exists. No wait or timer is added.

## 0.0.6

- T22.5-9: the tag v0.0.6 is created on the commit that holds T22.5-5-1, T22.5-7 and T22.5-8, so their entries move from `## Unreleased` into this section.

- T22.5-8: the limits of the tests in `tests/scripts` are about 100 times the expected time of their test, and none is below 20 times it. The template-ts reader test had the 30 s runner default, which its measured 28.6 s to 37.9 s came close to under load; its limit is 3,300 s, 100 times the mean of three solo runs. The other limits below 20 times their measured time are corrected: `atomic-publish` 690 s (measured 6.9 s), `browser-port` 820 s (8.2 s), `browser-server` 980 s (9.8 s), `package-installs` 4,980 s (49.8 s), the Rust runner of `generated-conformance` 6,230 s (62.3 s), `make test-rust` in `cargo-target` 9,180 s (91.8 s), `clean-release` 260 s and 530 s (2.6 s and 5.3 s), `conformance-progress` 430 s (4.2 s), `toolchain-files` 240 s (2.4 s), `long-steps` 210 s, 370 s and 830 s (2.1 s, 3.7 s and 8.3 s), the reader and unchanged tests of the build packages `template-language` (1,670 s and 650 s), `template-lsp` (600 s and 340 s), `template-codemirror` (1,110 s and 1,110 s) and `template-ts` (1,160 s unchanged), and the command deadline of `process-group-callers` 60 s (0.6 s). Deadlines that a test expects to fire are not pass limits and keep their values.

- T22.5-7: EPERM from a process group probe means a member is in exit, not that the group ended or that another user owns its ID. `signalGroup` in `scripts/process-group.mjs` returns `exiting` for EPERM and sends no further signal; `stopProcessGroup` waits until ESRCH, and rejects with the pgid, errno and elapsed time when EPERM persists past 10 s. Before this change the wait ended at the first EPERM, so the callers stopped while a member still answered `kill(pid, 0)`, which made `process-group-callers` fail on a loaded machine. `tests/runner/bounded.mjs` and the VS Code integration step signal through `signalGroup`; the `run-tests` timeout signal remains in the vendored kit.

- T22.5-5-1: the driver tests copy every module that `tests/runner/drivers.mjs` imports, including `scripts/python-toolchain.mjs`, from one list in `tests/scripts/driver-fixture.mjs`; `make test-scripts` passes again.

- T22.5-6: correct the records of 0.0.6. The ruff 0.17.0 declaration is recorded under T22.5-4, and the T22.5-5 entry moves from `## Unreleased` into this section; `## Unreleased` stays above it, empty, because the changelog check requires it as the first section. `docs/operations/development.md` names ruff 0.17.0 as declared in `config/toolchain.json` (installed by `make install-tools TOOLS="python ruff"` on `var/tools/bin/python3`, with the rules and target of `[tool.ruff]` in `packages/template-python/pyproject.toml`) in place of 0.16.10 and the `dev` extra, which the file does not have.

- T22.5-5: every Python check runs the pinned interpreter `var/tools/bin/python3` of the `pythonBuild` release in `config/toolchain.json`, not the `python3.14` of `PATH`: `scripts/python-toolchain.mjs`, the conformance drivers, `scripts/check-compiler-interface.mjs` and the `PYTHON` of the Makefile. `make install` installs npm, Go and that Python, `make install-tools TOOLS="python ruff"` installs ruff on it, and no workflow sets up a Python with `actions/setup-python`. Before this change, `make install-tools TOOLS=ruff` failed on a checkout without `var/tools/bin/python3`.

- T22.5-4: release 0.0.6. Every manifest of `config/release.json` declares 0.0.6, and the locks, the compiled artifacts, the dependency review and the consumer projects of the release assets record it.

- T22.5-4: `config/toolchain.json` declares `ruff` 0.17.0 (from 0.16.10) with the sha256 values of its wheels for darwin-arm64, darwin-x64, linux-arm64 and linux-x64. `make install-tools TOOLS="python ruff"` installs it.

- T22.5-3: the Python package is released as its wheel `polyspec_template-0.0.6-py3-none-any.whl` (PEP 427), built with the build backend of `buildBackend` in `config/release.json` (`setuptools==84.0.0`, sha256 checked) and installed by `consumers.wheel` with the import `polyspec.template`. Before this change, `make release-assets TAG=v0.0.6` failed with `config/release.json: proof.gitTag.packages/template-python/pyproject.toml.kind is "python", the allowed values are rust`; after it, the assets hold the wheel and `make release-consumer TAG=v0.0.6` installs and imports it. The package declares no dependency, so the wheel has no Requires-Dist. `requires-python` is `>=3.14`, the minor of `.python-version`; the CI job `python` runs that minor only, and the ruff pin is in `config/toolchain.json`.

- T22.5-2: the shared tools are the vendored copy of `polyspec/kit` v0.0.21. `config/toolchain.json` declares `pythonBuild` (Python 3.14.8, release 20261009), `pythonBackend` (`setuptools==84.0.0`) and `ruff` (0.16.10) with their sha256 values, and `config/dependency-policy.json` names its `npmLocks`, which the kit schema requires.
## 0.0.5

- T22.5-1: release 0.0.5. Every manifest of `config/release.json` declares 0.0.5, and the locks, the compiled artifacts, the dependency review and the consumer projects of the release assets record it.

- T22.4-34: `scripts/check-function-contract.mjs` reads the Python registry with either quote and a line break after `BuiltIn(`. `ruff format` (T22.4-21) wrote the keys in double quotes, so the checker found no Python function and `make function-contract-registries-check` failed in the CI job `scripts`.

- T22.4-33: `make install` installs npm and Go only (`install-tools TOOLS="npm go"`), the CI job `showcase` installs ruff after `setup-python`, `make dependency-review` installs the Rust toolchain before cargo-audit, its workflow sets up Python, and `make test-python` runs the `python3` of `PATH` again. The run of the push `a142548` failed in every job: ruff needed `python3.14` in jobs without `setup-python`, and cargo-audit needed the Rust toolchain before `make install` had installed it.

- T22.4-32: the shared tools are the vendored copy of `polyspec/kit` v0.0.8, whose `make install-tools TOOLS="npm go"` installs only the named tools.

- T22.4-31: the shared tools are the vendored copy of `polyspec/kit` v0.0.7, whose dependency gate accepts a local npm package required as `file:<its directory>`.

- T22.4-30: the shared tools are the vendored copy of `polyspec/kit` v0.0.6.

- T22.4-29: the compiled AST manifests, the generated JavaScript manifests and `examples/site/index.html` are regenerated, because the compiler digest covers `package-lock.json`, which `@playwright/test` 1.64.0 changed (T22.4-28).

- T22.4-22: `config/release.json` releases the Python package by git tag and declares its install from the tag (`pip install` of `polyspec-template` into a new virtual environment and an import of `polyspec.template`), which `make release-proof` runs after the release exists. `tests/scripts/release-config.test.mjs` checks the package names of the configuration against the manifests. The consumer projects of `tests/fixtures/release-consumer` and their locks name the archives `<package>-<language>-<version>.<ext>` (`polyspec-template-npm-0.0.4.tgz`, `polyspec-template-php-0.0.4.zip`), written by `make release-consumer-lock TAG=v0.0.4`.

- T22.4-21: `make lint-python` runs `ruff check` and `ruff format --check` on `packages/template-python` with `ruff==0.16.10`, pinned in the `dev` extra of its `pyproject.toml` and declared by `ruff.pyproject` of `config/toolchain.json`, and is in `CHECK_TARGETS` and the CI job `showcase`. The package had 21 unused imports and 36 files that `ruff format` changed; both are fixed with no change to its statements. `make test-python` runs the `python3` of `PATH`, or `PYTHON`. The review record holds `ruff==0.16.10`.

- T22.4-28: `config/dependency-policy.json` has the shape of kit, `config/toolchain.json` declares `cargoAudit` 0.22.2 and `govulncheck` 1.8.0, `@playwright/test` is 1.64.0, and `config/dependency-review.json` records the review of the npm, Composer and Cargo locks and of `setuptools==84.0.0`. `make dependency-policy-check` and `make dependency-policy-mutation-check` pass.

- T22.4-27: the shared tools are the vendored copy of `polyspec/kit` v0.0.4 (`scripts/kit/`, `tests/kit/`, `kit.json`, `.kit/kit.lock.json`). The repository copies of the dependency review and gate, the push gate, the full-run guard, the owner check, the release, the consumer install, `install-tools`, `ci-targets`, `ci-passed`, `target-report`, `run-tests`, `check-documents`, `holder-lock`, `git-hooks`, `github-ruleset` and `check-cargo-downloads` and their tests are removed, and `config/` holds the declarations of kit. Every document pair holds a `doc-id`, and the Korean file holds the `source-sha256` of the English file. Version 0.x has no pull request, merge queue or ruleset: `.github/ruleset.json` and `push-gate.yml` are removed, `ci.yml` runs on a push to `main` and a manual run, and `release.yml` requires the check `ci-passed`. `config/release.json` lists the packages of the release, whose assets are named `<package>-<language>-<version>.<ext>`.

- T22.4-20-9: the features pages and the showcase artifacts are regenerated, so the contract digests of the committed artifacts name the compiler of the tree.

- T22.4-7: a unit test of `tests/scripts/toolchain-files.test.mjs` checks that the `python` job of `ci.yml` runs `test-python`, `conformance-python` and `conformance-generated-python` and that `ci-passed` needs it; T22.4-19 and T22.4-19-1 are verified by their commands.
- Regenerated the compiled AST manifests, the generated manifests and `examples/site/index.html` after the interface change, so their contract digests match the contract (T22.4-24-1).
- T22.4-24: `packages/template-compiler/interface.json` declares the Python language mapping (backend, program, error, runtime services, `RuntimeEnvironment` fields and operations, `Frame` and `Scope` fields and operations, and the bound map) and `python` is again in `supportLevels.core`. `packages/template-python/tests/test_compiler_interface.py` compares these declarations with the package, `scripts/check-compiler-interface.mjs` runs it and checks `backends/python.mjs`, and `scripts/check-compiler-interface-mutations.mjs` rejects five Python mapping mutations. The package gains `RuntimeServices`, the class that `RuntimeEnvironment` implements. `docs/spec/compiler.md` names Python among the generated backends.

- T22.4-18: Python joins the language test matrix: `verification_matrix.languages` of `contracts/features.json` and `scripts/check-language-test-matrix.mjs` name `python` with the other four languages, and the six semantic features declare python `pass`. The Python verification of `ast-schema`, `template-function-contract` and `object-and-class-calls` is `make conformance-python` and `make test-python`. New Python tests: `test_function_contract.py` (registry names and argument ranges, arity errors, call syntax of `functions.json`), `test_object_calls.py` (the `tests/fixtures/native-object` fixtures and `host-values.json`: member and class calls, host arguments, native object equality) and `test_expr_fixtures.py` (tokens, AST, errors and values of `tests/fixtures/expr/cases.json`). Two defects found by them are fixed: a class instance returned by a host function, such as `pick(order)`, failed with `E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE` instead of binding as a native object, and a variadic built-in called with too few arguments raised `OverflowError` (`E_INTERNAL`) instead of `E_RUNTIME_ARITY`. `docs/spec/data-model.md` gains the Python binding table (VAL-23), the Python rows of VAL-19 and VAL-21.
- `scripts/check-package-installs.mjs` runs Python: an install project outside the checkout builds the wheel of a copy of `packages/template-python` with `pip wheel --no-index`, installs only that wheel into a new virtual environment and renders the scenario `scope-precedence` in AST mode and with the committed generated Python program. `make install` downloads the build backend that `packages/template-python/build-requirements.json` pins by version and SHA-256 digest into `var/python/wheelhouse` (`scripts/python-wheelhouse.mjs`), so the check reads no index. `contracts/features.json` declares the Python run of `make generated-native-check`, `make typed-generator-compile-check` and `make showcase-check`, and the features `showcase`, `object-and-class-calls` and `bound-data` are `partial` for Python (T22.4-20-5, T22.4-20).
- `scripts/check-typed-generator.mjs` runs Python: the committed generated Python programs of the five scenarios parse, render the scenario through `Engine` with the bytes of `expected.html` and keep a positioned `E_RUNTIME_LIMIT` template error when the output limit is exceeded. `Output.write` of the Python package called the limit callback, which returns the error, without raising it, so a render beyond `outputBytes` returned its whole output; it raises the error now, and `test_output_limit_is_a_positioned_template_error` holds it (T22.4-20-7).
- The showcase adapter contract has a Python implementation: `interface.json` maps `python`, `scripts/generate-showcase-contract.mjs` writes `generated/render_adapter.py`, `tools/showcase/adapters/python.py` implements `Adapter` over `Engine` with `AstProgram` or the generated program, and `tools/showcase/compile-generated.mjs` writes `generated/typed/<scenario>.py` with its manifest. `scripts/check-showcase-contract.mjs` parses the Python sources and runs the adapter on the five scenarios in AST and generated mode. The compiled AST manifests, the generated manifests and `examples/site/index.html` are regenerated for the changed contract digest (T22.4-20-6).
- The generated Python program extends `Program` and reads `RenderOptions` (`options.define`, `options.env`), so `Engine(GeneratedProgram()).render(...)` renders it like `AstProgram`; it read a `dict` and failed with `'RenderOptions' object has no attribute 'get'`. The generated Python conformance runner and the Python entries of the generated checks pass `RenderOptions` (T22.4-20-8).
- `scripts/check-generated-native-calls.mjs` runs Python: the generated Python program calls members and class functions of an assigned host object, passes the object unchanged to included templates, blocks and definition data, and reports the error matrix and the host argument values as the other languages do (T22.4-20-4).
- `scripts/check-generated-bound-data.mjs` runs Python: a generated Python program renders a bound map as assign and as definition data with the bytes of the host map. The Python backend read the member of a typed record as an attribute, so `{= m.k}` failed with `'dict' object has no attribute 'k'`; it reads through `_generated_member`, which returns null for a null record and an absent optional field (T22.4-20-3).
- `scripts/check-generated-arguments.mjs` runs Python. A request whose `assign` or definition `data` does not match the declared types of a generated Python program now fails with a `ValueError`, the argument error of Python; `GeneratedProgram.prepare` bound the request inside `internal_boundary`, which reported it as the template error `E_INTERNAL` (ERR-13). `render` keeps the boundary, and `docs/spec/errors.md` names the Python argument error (T22.4-20-2).
- `scripts/check-generated-typed-values.mjs` runs the generated Python program of `tests/fixtures/typed-values` with the interpreter `python<minor>` of `.python-version` (`scripts/python-toolchain.mjs`). The Python backend emitted `_generated_bind_assign` for a dynamic root also when the type manifest declares a typed root, so every field of `Assign` was unset and a typed template failed with `in requires a list, map or string on the right`; `emitRuntime` now uses `program.dynamicRoot`. The CI jobs `scripts`, `generated` and `showcase` set up Python from `.python-version`, and `tests/scripts/python-check-coverage.test.mjs` fails for a check without a Python entry (T22.4-20-1).
- T22.4-19-1: the Python coverage check reads the verification commands that declare a Python run (`"python": true` in `contracts/features.json`), not the words of the Makefile and the scripts; `template-function-contract` declares python unsupported, since its command runs no Python.

- The features page shows the Python status of every feature: `scripts/features/build.mjs` and `check.mjs` list `python` among the clients, so `docs/features.md` and `docs/features.ko.md` carry the Python column (T22.4-19).
- The language test matrix in `contracts/features.json` lists the four languages that `scripts/check-language-test-matrix.mjs` requires; Python joins it when the CI merge-group run of T22.4-7 passes (T22.4-13).
- Declared the `py.typed` marker of `polyspec.template` under its package name in `packages/template-python/pyproject.toml`; the key `template` named no package under `src`. `tests/test_package_data.py` checks every package-data key and runs in `make test-python` (T22.4-14).
- Documented the Python release form: the package `polyspec-template` is consumed by git tag like the Rust crate, and `AGENTS.md` lists its `pyproject.toml` among the version-bump manifests (T22.4-15).
- Ran the three Python unit tests of `make test-python` in one loop that keeps the failure of each test, so the target runs one command as `tests/scripts/failure-accumulation.test.mjs` requires (T22.4-16).
- Regenerated the compiled AST manifests, the generated manifests and `examples/site/index.html` after the contract change, so their contract digests match the contract (T22.4-17).
- Removed `python` from the core support level of `packages/template-compiler/interface.json`, because the level named python without its language mapping; the core interface check and the bound map mutation check of the `showcase` job pass again (T22.4-23).
- Regenerated the compiled AST manifests, the generated manifests and `examples/site/index.html` after the interface change, so their contract digests match the contract (T22.4-25).
- `tests/scripts/toolchain-files.test.mjs` fails when the `python` job matrix of `ci.yml` omits 3.11 or the minor of `.python-version` (T22.4-12).
- Reformatted the Python package unit test file `packages/template-python/tests/test_api.py` with no change to its statements (T22.4-11).
- `polyspec.template.__all__` exports `ParseOptions`, `analyze` and `analyze_prefix`, lists `PreparedRender` once, and exports every name that the documents import (T22.4-10).
- Set the Python support of each feature in `contracts/features.json` from the Python coverage of its verification commands: 18 features are `unsupported`, and the 5 features whose commands run Python are `partial` until the CI merge-group run of T22.4-7 passes (T22.4-9).
- Pinned `actions/setup-python` in the `python` job of `ci.yml` to the commit of its `v6.3.0` tag, and added `tests/scripts/action-pins.test.mjs`, which checks every action pin of every workflow against the commit of its version comment (T22.4-8).
- Documented the Python package in `README.md`, `docs/guide.md` and the package README (T22.4-6). The
  README lists Python among the implementations, the guide gives a render example that uses the
  names of `polyspec.template`, and the package README names `vX.Y.Z` as the install tag until a
  release tag contains `packages/template-python`.

  functions and the command line interface as the package `polyspec-template` under the import name
  `polyspec.template`; the conformance and parity runners, the delimiter matrix, the function
  contract and the CI cover it like every other language, and the compiler emits Python for a

  generated conformance on 3.11 and the pinned minor release, and the release version check reads
  `packages/template-python/pyproject.toml` with the other manifests.
- Updated `@codemirror/language` to 6.13.1 (T22.3-6). 6.13.1 declares `@codemirror/streamparser`, which 6.13.0 imported without declaring it, so `config/dependency-policy.json` no longer keeps 6.12.4 with an exception; `make dependency-review UPDATE=1 RECORD=1` updated `package.json` and `package-lock.json` and recorded the review, and `make showcase` compiled the committed artifacts again for the lock.

## 0.0.4

- Released 0.0.4 (T22.3-5). Every manifest of `MANIFESTS` of `scripts/release.mjs`, `@polyspec/template-compiler` included, the `version` of each `composer.json`, the dependencies between the npm packages, the version of the C extension, the locks and the consumer projects of `tests/fixtures/release-consumer` declare `0.0.4`; `make showcase` compiled the committed artifacts again for the compiler digest of 0.0.4, `make release-consumer-lock` wrote the locks of the consumer projects and `make dependency-review RECORD=1` recorded the changed locks.
- The documentation links the compiler's `interface.json` and `functions.json` at their repository location, and the Rust compiler interface test is formatted (T22.3-4).
- Published the compiler as the npm package `@polyspec/template-compiler` (T22.3-3). `packages/template-compiler` holds the compiler modules, the backends, `interface.json` and the function contract `functions.json`, and exports `compiler.mjs`, `ast-artifact.mjs`, `type-manifest.mjs` and `functions.json`; it depends on `@polyspec/template` by exact version, and `ast-artifact.mjs` imports the parser from it. The compiler digest names the files of the package by their paths in the package and, installed, reads the installed `@polyspec/template`. `make release-assets` packs `polyspec-template-compiler-X.Y.Z.tgz`, and the npm consumer project of `tests/fixtures/release-consumer` installs it with `npm ci`, imports its entry points and `functions.json` and compiles a template with it. The dependency review of the changed lock raised `@codemirror/view` to 6.43.14; `config/dependency-policy.json` keeps `@codemirror/language` at 6.12.4, because 6.13.0 imports `@codemirror/streamparser` without declaring it.

## 0.0.3

- Installed every npm package of the release from the built archives (T22.3-2). The npm consumer project of `tests/fixtures/release-consumer` installs `@polyspec/template`, `@polyspec/template-language`, `@polyspec/template-lsp` and `@polyspec/template-codemirror` with `npm ci`, and its lock pins their registry dependencies by exact version and integrity; `scripts/release-consumer.mjs` packs the built packages of the tree with the step of `make release-assets`, and the locks record the archives under test by name and version only. AGENTS and the Makefile state that a registry query whose result depends on time never runs in a check and that a download pinned by a lock is installation, which a check may run.
- Installed the release assets from committed consumer projects (T22.3-1). `tests/fixtures/release-consumer` holds an npm project and a Composer project with their locks, which `make release-consumer-lock` writes; `tests/scripts/release.test.mjs` installs the packed assets with them outside the repository through `npm ci` with an empty cache and the scope `@polyspec` on an unreachable registry and through `composer install` with an empty `COMPOSER_HOME` and cache. `make release-assets` archives each Composer package at the time of the tagged commit, so its zip is the same bytes on every run, and AGENTS states that a registry query whose result depends on time never runs in a check while a download pinned by a lock is installation. The nine cases of `tests/scripts/release.test.mjs` that the change of T22.2-3 removed run again.
- Released 0.0.3 (T22.3). Every manifest of `MANIFESTS` of `scripts/release.mjs`, the `version` of each `composer.json`, the dependencies between the npm packages, the version of the C extension and the locks declare `0.0.3`; `make showcase` compiled the committed artifacts again for the compiler digest of 0.0.3, and `make dependency-review RECORD=1` recorded the changed locks.
- Made the release assets install outside the repository (T22.2-3). Each published `composer.json` declares the version of the release, which an `artifact` repository of Composer reads, and `make release-versions` requires it. Each archive carries the manifest of its package unchanged, and `make release-assets` fails unless every packed manifest equals its manifest at the tagged commit and names each `@polyspec/*` and `polyspec/*` dependency by an exact version, not a path, a git source, a URL, a range or `@dev`, with no `repositories`; `tests/scripts/release.test.mjs` applies the same rules to the published manifests of the tree and installs the packed assets in a directory outside the repository with `npm install` from an empty cache and with Composer. `docs/operations/publication.md` states the install from the release assets with `file:` tarballs and with an `artifact` or `package` repository, and the development layout.
- Kept the release notes within the limit of GitHub (T22.2-2). GitHub refuses a release body over 125000 characters, and `make release-publish` passed the whole section `## X.Y.Z` of `CHANGELOG.md`; a section over `NOTES_LIMIT` (125000 characters) of `scripts/release.mjs` is replaced by one line that links the section of `CHANGELOG.md` at the tag, and a section at the limit stays whole. `tests/scripts/release.test.mjs` checks both and the link of a Go tag.

## 0.0.2

- Compiled the committed artifacts and reviewed the dependencies again for 0.0.2 (T22.2-1). The compiler digest covers the version of `packages/template-ts`, so `make showcase` compiled the AST and JavaScript manifests again; the review record names the sha256 of `package-lock.json`, so `make dependency-review UPDATE=1` raised `typescript-eslint` to 8.71.1 and `source-map-js` to 1.2.2, which fixes advisory GHSA-68fv-2mgg-jv7q, and recorded the review. `scripts/dependency-review.mjs` read `npm view --json` as one object, and npm 12 prints several fields as an array of one object, so every npm dependency failed with no stable release; the review reads both forms, and the npm stub of `tests/scripts/dependency-policy.test.mjs` answers in the form of npm 12. The gate test changes the version of `packages/template-lsp` to a version that differs from the tree.
- Released 0.0.2 (T22.2). Every manifest of `MANIFESTS` of `scripts/release.mjs`, the dependencies between the npm packages, the version of the C extension and the locks declare `0.0.2`. `scripts/check-package-installs.mjs` reads the archive of the Rust crate under the version of `packages/template-rust/Cargo.toml`, the stubs of `npm pack` and `cargo package` in `tests/scripts/package-installs.test.mjs` name their archives after the version of the packed manifest, and `tests/scripts/release.test.mjs` checks the version of the root `package.json`, so no check names a fixed version.
- Ran each target of the full suite in exactly one CI job (T17.1-10). The jobs `scripts`, `generated`, `delimiters` and `showcase` of `ci.yml` run the targets that no other job runs with `make ci-targets`, in place of the job `release`, which ran `make check` and every target of the other jobs again; `CHECK_TARGETS` names the six lint targets and the eight conformance targets that the jobs run, and `tests/scripts/suite-parity.test.mjs` fails unless the targets of the `make ci-targets` steps are `CHECK_TARGETS` with each target in one job. `make ci-targets` writes the toolchain versions of its job, the PHP patch included, into `toolchains.json` and `summary.md` of its report.
- Released the Go module tags at any depth and only npm and Composer archives (T22.1-5). The trigger of `.github/workflows/release.yml` is `tags: ['v*', '**/v*']`: in a tag filter `*` does not match `/`, so `**/v*` covers `packages/template-go/vX.Y.Z`. The release assets are npm tarballs and Composer zips only: the Rust crate `packages/template-rust` is not released as an archive; it is consumed by git tag, because `cargo package` rewrites git dependencies into crates.io requirements that do not resolve, and `MANIFESTS` of `scripts/release.mjs` lists it so. A Go module tag builds and attaches nothing, and `make release-assets` builds the npm packages only for a tag without `/`. `tests/scripts/release.test.mjs` fails on a Cargo archive, and `tests/scripts/toolchain-files.test.mjs` on the trigger `*/v*`.
- Released a tag of a commit of `main` through a workflow (T22.1-4). `.github/workflows/release.yml`, which is not the job `release` of `ci.yml`, runs on the push of a tag `v*` or `**/v*` with the permission `contents: write`; `make release-verify` requires the tagged commit on `origin/main` with the check runs `push-gate` and `ci-passed` concluded `success`, `make release-versions` the version of the tag in every manifest and the section `## X.Y.Z` in `CHANGELOG.md`, `make release-assets` builds the npm and Composer archives, and `make release-publish` creates the GitHub Release with the section as notes (`scripts/release.mjs`, `tests/scripts/release.test.mjs`). AGENTS states the release procedure.
- Required exactly the checks `push-gate` and `ci-passed` on `main` (T22.1-3). `ci-passed`, the last job of `ci.yml`, needs every other job, runs after each of them under `if: ${{ always() }}` and runs `make ci-passed` (`scripts/ci-passed.mjs`), which fails unless every needed job has the result `success`; a job added to `ci.yml` is required once it is in `needs`. `tests/scripts/toolchain-files.test.mjs` fails when the job is missing, is not last, lacks `if: ${{ always() }}`, does not need every other job, runs on another runner or runs another step.
- Named the Go module after its directory (T22.1-2). `packages/template-go/go.mod` declared `github.com/polyspec/template`, which `go get` looks for at the root of the repository, where no `go.mod` is; the module is `github.com/polyspec/template/packages/template-go`, every import, the Go backend of the compiler, the generated Go programs with their manifests, the example page and the documents use it, and a tag `packages/template-go/vX.Y.Z` releases it. `tests/scripts/toolchain-files.test.mjs` fails when a tracked `go.mod` with a domain does not name its directory.
- Kept the section `## Unreleased` above the released versions (T22.1-1). The entries of the tag `v0.0.1` form the section `## 0.0.1`, the entries after it stay under `## Unreleased`, and every change adds its entry there; the language link of the changelog stands below its title.
- Declared the triggers of each workflow exactly (T17.1-9). `ci.yml` runs on every pull request, merge group and manual run (`workflow_dispatch`), and `pages.yml` deploys the documentation site on a push to `main` and on a manual run.

## 0.0.1

- Published every change through a pull request and the merge queue (T17.1-7).
- Caught stale compiled artifacts before `main` (T17.1-6). The AST compiler digest is computed from the tracked sources of `packages/template-ts` instead of its build, and `make artifact-digest-check` fails, naming each manifest and `make showcase`, when a committed artifact records the digest of another compiler; the job `push-gate` and `showcase-check` run it. The 30 artifacts and the page are compiled again for the new digest.
- Regenerated the showcase artifacts after T8.5-1 (T8.5-2). The AST artifact manifests record a digest of the template-ts build, which T8.5-1 changed, so the release job found `examples/site/index.html` stale; the five manifests and the page are written again, and only the digests change.
- Failed `json` of a native object in every runtime (T8.5-1). The PHP runtime raised `E_INTERNAL` and the other runtimes wrote `null`; FUN-26 states that a value that is or holds a native object has no JSON text and fails with E_RUNTIME_TYPE at the call, and the shared fixture `tests/fixtures/native-object/json.tpl` checks it in every runtime, the extension and the generated programs.
- Kept the inputs of a run in memory across `setjmp` (T21.4-2). GCC still warned with `-Wclobbered` for two parameters of `Engine::render`; every run goes through `pt_run_work`, which takes a pointer to a structure of its inputs.
- Ran the document, checklist, feature and rule checks in the job `push-gate` (T17.1-5), the check that the ruleset `main` requires, so a commit that breaks them cannot reach `main`; they need Node.js alone, which a test of `tests/scripts/push-gate.test.mjs` requires.
- Ran every combination of a CI matrix to its end (T20.1-13). The job PHP 8.2 failed and GitHub cancelled the job PHP 8.5; the php job of `ci.yml` sets `fail-fast: false`, and `tests/scripts/toolchain-files.test.mjs` requires it of every job with a matrix.
- Built the C extension with the GCC of the CI runner (T21.4-1). GCC warned with `-Werror` that two locals of `Engine::render` might be clobbered by `longjmp` and that a local of `date` may be used uninitialized; the work of each run is a function of its own and every local filled through an out parameter starts initialized.
- Made a push impossible while a checklist task is `[~]` (T17.1-3). The tracked pre-push hook `.githooks/pre-push` runs `scripts/push-gate.mjs hook`, which refuses a push while the checklist of a pushed commit or of the working tree has a task in progress, naming each task; every make invocation sets `core.hooksPath` to `.githooks`, `make hooks` and `make hooks-check` install and check it, and the guard of `make check` refuses without it. The workflow `.github/workflows/push-gate.yml` runs the same gate on every push and pull request through `make ci-targets TARGETS="push-gate-commit"` with pinned actions on `ubuntu-24.04`, uploads its report and cancels the running gate of its ref on a new push.
- Replaced the Rust PHP extension with the C extension (T21.4). `make ext` builds the C sources into `var/build/polyspec_template.so`, `test-ext` and the `php-ext` driver of the conformance runner use that build, and the Rust sources, the cargo steps (`lint-ext`, `test-ext-clippy`, the crate fetch of `make install`) and the ext-php-rs dependency are removed. The stub moved to `src/`, `composer.json` has the PIE type `php-ext`, `CliTest` loads the published library, and the Rust panic boundary test is removed.
- Bound PHP values in the C extension (T21.3). `Engine::render` binds assign data, definitions and environment given as PHP values and takes a bound map without binding it again; `register` and `registerClass` register host and class functions, which receive the host form of VAL-21; native objects keep their instance and expose only public members; `BoundMap::bind` and `merge` create bound maps that cannot be instantiated, cloned or unserialized. The extension tests except the command line test pass unchanged on the C build.
- Rendered templates with JSON data in the C extension (T21.2). `Engine::renderJson` reads assign data, definitions and environment as JSON text with the rules of VAL-2, VAL-12 and VAL-20 and renders with the filesystem loader and its version cache, every statement, expression and limit and every built-in function; `json` of a native object writes `null` like the TypeScript and Rust runtimes. All 248 conformance cases pass.
- Built the PHP extension in C and parsed templates with it (T21.1). `packages/template-php-ext/src` holds the C sources, `config.m4` and the stub `polyspec_template.stub.php` with `TemplateError` and `Engine::__construct`, `parse` and `parseToJson`; `make ext-arginfo` generates the committed arginfo header from the stub with gen_stub.php. `scripts/build-php-extension.mjs` builds the extension with phpize, configure and make in a temporary directory with warnings as errors, publishes the library by rename when its bytes change, skips unchanged inputs and fails when the header does not match the stub. The parser gives the AST or the parse error of every conformance case. The Rust extension stays the extension of `make ext` until T21.4.
- Let a new push cancel the running CI of its workflow and ref (T20.1-12): `ci.yml` groups its runs by `<workflow>-<ref>` with `cancel-in-progress: true`, and a case of `tests/scripts/toolchain-files.test.mjs` requires that of every workflow that runs on push or pull request.
- Fixed the failures of CI on T20.1-9 (T20.1-11). `make ci-targets` failed in every job with `ENOENT` on its lock, because a new checkout has no `var/report`; the report test expected no `Entering directory` line of GNU Make 4; and inline code with `{{` in the checklist failed the rendering of two pages while the build passed. `scripts/ci-targets.mjs` creates `var/report` before the lock, the test accepts GNU Make 4, and `scripts/check-documents.mjs` fails on a Vue interpolation outside a code block of a page under docs.
- Stated that development runs unit tests only (T20.1-10). AGENTS required `make owner-check` before every commit and `make check` before a push; AGENTS and `docs/operations/development.md` now state that no full or end-to-end check runs locally before a commit or a push, and that CI runs them after the push with a report of every failure.
- Left the reason of every failure of CI (T20.1-9). Only the release job uploaded its record, and no job kept the output of a target beyond the job log. `make check` and `make ci-targets` now write the log of each target and a summary with the first failure lines of each failed target to `var/report`, also as the job summary, every CI job runs its checks through them and uploads the report under `!cancelled()`, and tests check the report of a failing target and the steps of every job.
- Ran every tool of CI through make (T20.1-8). Seven steps of `ci.yml` started node directly, the Playwright install and the conformance of the language jobs, outside the offline settings of the recipes and `cargo-downloads-check`. `make install-browsers` and `make conformance-ts`, `conformance-go`, `conformance-rust` and `conformance-php` run them, and a case of `tests/scripts/toolchain-files.test.mjs` fails on a workflow step that starts a tool without make.
- Waited for the extension in the VS Code integration checks without a time limit of their own (T20.1-7). `eventually` gave up after 5 s and returned `false`, so under load the check of format on type failed in 1 of 5 runs with `+ false` and no text. `tests/integration/suite/wait.cjs` polls until the condition holds, only the 20 s timeout of the check ends a wait and names what it waited for with the expected and the actual text, and the checks compare the text of the document; `tests/integration-wait.test.mjs` applies a format after 5.5 s, which failed with the former wait and passes now. The new message showed the cause under load: the document had none of the typed text, because the command `type` types into the focused editor; the check now waits until its editor is the active one before it types.
- Installed VS Code only where the integration test runs (T20.1-6). `make install` downloaded VS Code in all nine CI jobs; `make install-vscode` now installs it after `make install`, the release and editor jobs run it before the integration test, and a case of `tests/scripts/toolchain-files.test.mjs` ties the jobs that reach `test-vscode-integration` to that step and fails on any other job that runs it.
- Named `make install` for a missing crate in every target that runs cargo (T20.1-5). The recipes run cargo offline, and a missing crate failed with cargo's advice to retry without `--offline`. `make cargo-downloads-check` runs `cargo fetch --locked --offline` for every tracked Cargo.lock and fails with each lock, the first error line of cargo and `run make install, which downloads them`; the 26 targets that run cargo depend on it, and `tests/scripts/cargo-downloads.test.mjs` fails on a target that runs cargo without it.
- Installed the VS Code of the integration test in `make install` (T20.1-4). `tests/integration/run.mjs` downloaded VS Code 1.138.0 into `.vscode-test` on its first run, a download of 302 MB in a check. `scripts/install-vscode.mjs` downloads the copy into `var/tools/vscode/.next-<pid>` and renames it into place, and does nothing for a present copy; `make install` runs it, and the integration run starts only the installed copy and fails with `run make install` without it. The installed copy never changes, so the holder lock of `.vscode-test`, `make clean-vscode-test` and `make vscode-test-unlock` are removed; `tests/scripts/install-vscode.test.mjs` checks the install and the failure.
- Installed the Composer packages in `make install` and ran Composer offline in the checks (T20.1-3). `build-php` and `build-ext-php` ran `composer install`, which read Packagist when `vendor` was absent. `scripts/composer-install.mjs` runs `composer install` and, when it fails with the network disabled, names `run make install`; `make install` runs it with the network for both PHP packages, the Makefile exports `COMPOSER_DISABLE_NETWORK=1`, and the recipes, the PHP driver and the tests that install PHPUnit run the script. With an empty `vendor` and Composer cache `make build-php` downloaded 24 MB before and fails with that message after, and it passes offline after the install step.
- Ran cargo, go and npm offline in every recipe (T20.1-2). The cargo commands of the checks ran without `--offline`, so with an empty `CARGO_HOME` a recipe reached crates.io, and go and npm could read their registries. The Makefile exports `CARGO_NET_OFFLINE=true`, `GOPROXY=off` and `npm_config_offline=true`; only the downloads of `install`, `install-tools` and `dependency-review` lift them with `$(ONLINE)`, and `tests/scripts/toolchain-files.test.mjs` asserts both. With an empty temporary `CARGO_HOME` a recipe's cargo fails offline before `cargo fetch --locked` and passes after it, and the cargo, go and npm checks pass offline. `composer install` of `build-php` and `ext` and the VS Code download of `test-vscode-integration` still read the network outside `make install`.
- Downloaded the crates of every Cargo.lock in `make install` (T20.1-1). `rustWorkspace` of `scripts/temporary-workspace.mjs` and the generated checks resolve their temporary crates with `cargo --offline`, which finds only crates that an earlier cargo command downloaded, so the Rust runner case of `tests/scripts/generated-conformance.test.mjs` failed in CI with `no matching package named serde_json found` when it ran before any other cargo, and fails every time with an empty `CARGO_HOME`. `make install` runs `cargo fetch --locked` for every tracked Cargo.lock, the checks stay offline, a failed `cargo metadata --offline` names `run make install`, and `rustWorkspace` removes its directory when it fails; `tests/scripts/toolchain-files.test.mjs` asserts the fetches. With an empty temporary `CARGO_HOME`, `rustWorkspace` fails before the fetch and passes after it.
- Gave the probe makefile of `tests/scripts/failure-accumulation.test.mjs` as a file of the run (T19.8-1). The test passed it as `-f /dev/stdin` with the `input` of `spawnSync`, which Node.js passes through a socket; macOS duplicates the descriptor, but Linux opens `/proc/self/fd/0` and cannot open a socket, so in CI make failed with `/dev/stdin: No such device or address` and ran no probe target. The test writes the probe into a temporary directory outside the checkout, asserts the output of the probe targets before the status and names the probe file and the stderr of make; AGENTS states the rule, and `tests/scripts/device-paths.test.mjs`, which runs for every change, fails on a device path in a tracked source.
- Stated the rules of idempotency in AGENTS (T19.15): a check gives the same result for the same tree at any time and on any machine, and a section names one rule for each class of defect that Wave 19 fixed, with the test that checks it where one exists: toolchains declared by the checkout and installed into `var/tools`, no network in a check, builds that a check makes itself and publishes only when they change, run-owned temporary files, caches, locks and process groups, no run that verifies nothing, every failure in one run, owners for every input of a check, failure messages with expected and actual values or the fix, and tests independent of the output format of tools and of the user. `make docs-check` passes.
- Built Go into the build cache of the checkout (T19.14). `scripts/check-generated-native-calls.mjs`, `check-generated-typed-values.mjs`, `check-generated-arguments.mjs`, `check-generated-bound-data.mjs`, `check-typed-generator.mjs` and `check-compiler-interface.mjs` set `GOCACHE` to `/tmp/template-go-cache`, a fixed directory outside the checkout that every checkout and run of the machine shared and that the other Go commands of the recipes did not use; no requirement needs a shared cache. The Makefile exports `GOCACHE=var/go/cache`, the scripts use the environment of the recipe, and `make clean` removes `var/go`. The new case of `tests/scripts/toolchain-files.test.mjs` failed before the change, its 11 cases pass after it, and `make test-go` passes its 371 tests with the cache of the checkout.
- Left no process of a group that a check started (T19.13). `runBounded` and `runStep` of `tests/runner/bounded.mjs`, which the generated TypeScript and PHP conformance runners use, resolved when the command closed its output, and `runStep` of `packages/template-vscode/tests/integration/step.mjs` when VS Code exited, whose caller then removes the profile directories. A background process that the command started in its group without its output ran on: in the new `tests/scripts/process-group-callers.test.mjs` the background process still ran when `runBounded` resolved after the command exited, when `runStep` of the runners resolved and when `runStep` of the integration test resolved; only the deadline case of `runBounded` found it ended, in 30 of 30 probes. The three now resolve after `stopProcessGroup` (T20.3), moved from `tests/scripts` to `scripts/process-group.mjs`, stopped the rest of the group and found no process of it. The 4 cases of the new test pass, 3 of them failed before the change, and the cases of `tests/scripts/process-group.test.mjs`, `long-steps.test.mjs`, `generated-conformance.test.mjs` and the VS Code step tests pass.
- Locked the record of the full run (T19.12). `scripts/full-run.mjs` read `var/full-run.json`, decided and wrote the record without a lock: two guards that started together both found no running process, both ran the suite and wrote the record in turn, and a guard ran its targets while another guard held the record. The guard now takes the holder lock `var/full-run.json.lock` of `scripts/holder-lock.mjs` before it reads the record and releases it after its last write, also when a target throws; while another guard holds it, the guard refuses with the holder (`... is held by process <pid> of the checkout ..., started at ...; the guard of another run reads or writes var/full-run.json`) and runs no target. `acquire` of the holder lock left its `exit` and signal handlers registered after the release; the release removes them, so a process can take and release locks many times. The copy of the first documentation build of `make docs-verify-idempotent` has a directory of its run since T19.7. The refusal case of the 2 new cases of `tests/scripts/full-run.test.mjs` failed on the tree of T19.6-1, where the guard ran its target and wrote the record, and its 13 cases pass after the change with the 5 cases of `tests/scripts/holder-lock.test.mjs`.
- Ran the builds that changed instead of every new file of a build (T19.6-1). After T19.6 built the CLIs before every run, `make delimiter-matrix` failed in the owner check of T19.11: the first calls of the Go and Rust CLIs, `"!!" [go]`, `"!!" [rust]` and `"!\"" [go]`, outlived their 10 s limit, while the other 5,109 calls passed, at a load average of 50. cargo links `target/release/template` again on every build, also when nothing changed (a new inode on each of three builds), go build writes its output again, and macOS checks a new executable file on its first run: the first run of the Rust CLI after a build took 29.8 s, the next ones 0.00 s. The new `scripts/publish-build.mjs` publishes a build to `var/build` only when its bytes change, writing a temporary file with the mode of the build and renaming it; `make build-go`, `make build-rust` and `make ext` publish the Go and Rust CLIs and the PHP extension, the conformance drivers publish after their builds and run the published files, `make test-ext-unit` loads the published extension, and `tools/showcase/benchmark-modes.mjs` measures the published Go and Rust adapters. Two builds of the Rust CLI kept the inode of the published file, whose first run took 0.25 s. The new `tests/scripts/publish-build.test.mjs` checks that an unchanged build keeps the published file and a changed build replaces it.
- Made the tests independent of the output format of tools and of the user that runs them (T19.11). The stale-lock case of `tests/scripts/dependency-policy.test.mjs` matched `lock file is not up to date` in the output of composer; it now matches only the message of the gate with its fix. `scripts/check-package-installs.mjs` took the name of the archive from the last line of `npm pack --silent`: with an npm that prints a notice after the name, the install project installed `npm notice ...` and the case of `tests/scripts/package-installs.test.mjs` failed; the check computes `polyspec-template-0.0.1.tgz` from the name and version of the manifest and fails when npm wrote no such file, and the stub `npm install` of the test fails on a missing archive. The filesystem loader tests of TypeScript, Go, Rust, PHP and the PHP extension expected a file of mode 0o000 to fail with `E_LOAD_FAILED`, which is wrong for root, who reads it; each test now asserts that the process can read the file exactly when it runs as root and expects `E_LOAD_FAILED` for every other user and the rendered file for root. `scripts/check-install-workspace.mjs` made a removal fail with a directory of mode 0o555, which root removes; it now removes a workspace that no longer exists and expects the failure to name it. The changed cases pass: the Go, TypeScript, Rust, PHP and PHP extension loader tests, both cases of the install workspace check, and the 2 cases of `tests/scripts/package-installs.test.mjs`, which failed with the archive name of the tree of T19.10.
- Named what failed with the expected and the actual value or the fix (T19.10). The generated Go and Rust conformance runners reported a failed test run as `generated Go execution failed; the failed cases are listed above`; `runCaseTests` of `tests/runner/cases.mjs` now prints the output of `scripts/run-tests.mjs` as it arrives, reads the failure lines and names each failed case (`echo/path: the generated Go test of the case failed`), or the last line when the tests failed before a case, and the Rust runner counts the passed cases from it. `scripts/run-tests.mjs` ended with an unhandled `error` event when a tool could not start; it now prints, for example, `cannot start go: spawn go ENOENT; run make install-tools, which installs Go into var/tools`. A CLI call of `invoke` of `tests/runner/drivers.mjs` that outlived its limit returned the message of `spawnSync`; it now returns `<command> did not finish within its limit of <seconds> s`. The Go and PHP package install projects of `scripts/check-package-installs.mjs` failed with a message that named neither output; they now print which program differs with the expected and the actual output. `make test-ext` ran PHPUnit through `packages/template-php-ext/run-tests.sh`, which printed no test with its elapsed time and had no per-test timeout; `scripts/run-tests.mjs phpunit --php-extension <library>` now loads the built extension, and `make test-ext-unit` passed its 313 tests through it; the script and the `test` script of the Composer manifest were removed. The 5 cases of the new `tests/scripts/failure-messages.test.mjs` failed on the tree of T19.9 and pass after the change.
- Selected every check that reads a changed path (T19.9). `scripts/owner-checks.json` selected only the unit tests and ESLint for a change of a package, so a change of `packages/template-go` ran neither gofmt, the conformance, the generated Go conformance, the delimiter matrix nor the package install projects; a change of template-rust did not run `test-ext`, whose extension builds on it; a change of template-ts ran none of the language, LSP, CodeMirror, VS Code and browser tests that build on it; a change of `tools/compiler` ran neither the generated conformance, `generated-native-check` nor `showcase-check`; `scripts/check-benchmark-smoke.mjs` and `tools/showcase/benchmark-modes.mjs` did not select `benchmark-smoke`, `scripts/generate-showcase-contract.mjs`, which `docs-check` runs, did not select it, and the VS Code packages did not select `test-vscode-integration`. The rules now select them. `inputs` of the declaration names, for each check target, the globs of the paths that it reads, and `scripts/owner-check.mjs` fails, on every run and with `--validate`, on an input path whose rules select neither the target nor a target that runs it as a prerequisite, on an input of a target that the Makefile lacks and on an input glob without a path. With the declared inputs, the validation of the rules of T19.8 named 1,780 input paths of 29 targets that no rule selected for their target; the validation of the new rules names none. The 2 new cases of `tests/scripts/owner-check.test.mjs` failed before the change in the validation case and its 11 cases pass after it.
- Reported every failure of one run (T19.8). make stops a recipe at its first failed command, so a failure of `go vet` hid the Go tests, a failed document check hid the 9 checks after it in `make docs-check`, and 30 targets of the full suite and of its prerequisites ran more than one command; a run stopped at its first failed target, and `scripts/check-generated-native-calls.mjs`, `check-generated-typed-values.mjs`, `check-generated-bound-data.mjs`, `check-typed-generator.mjs` and `check-package-installs.mjs` stopped at their first failed language. The Makefile sets `MAKEFLAGS += -k`, and `scripts/full-run.mjs` runs `make -k <target>`; every target of the full suite and of its prerequisites runs at most one command, a target with several checks names them as prerequisites (for example `lint` runs `lint-js`, `lint-go`, `lint-rust`, `lint-ext`, `lint-showcase-format`, `lint-showcase-warnings` and `lint-php`, and `docs-check` reuses `schema-check`, `doc-coverage` and `feature-check`), and a step that needs an earlier one names it as its prerequisite, as `docs-static-check` names `docs`; the guard `require-dir`, whose packages all exist, was removed. The commands of the full suite are unchanged: `make -n` printed the same commands for every target of `CHECK_TARGETS` before and after the change, apart from the two type checks of `test-ts`, now two commands, and the sub-make of `showcase-check`, now a prerequisite. The language check scripts run every language through `checkLanguages` of the new `scripts/language-checks.mjs`, which prints each language with its result and fails after the last one with the languages that failed; `check-generated-arguments.mjs` collected command failures but stopped at an exception. The new `tests/scripts/failure-accumulation.test.mjs` failed in 3 of its 4 cases on the tree of T19.7 and passes after the change.
- Published the files and directories that others read at once, keeping the previous version on failure (T19.7). `make showcase` and `make bench` ran `node tools/showcase/benchmark-modes.mjs > examples/site/data/mode-benchmark.json`: the shell emptied the committed file before the measurement, so a reader found it empty and a failed run left it empty. `benchmark-modes.mjs --output <file>` now writes a temporary file next to the file and renames it, and both targets use it. `make docs-verify-idempotent` copied its first build to the fixed `docs/.vitepress/dist.first`, which two runs shared; the copy now goes into a directory of the run from `mktemp -d`, which the recipe removes also after a failed comparison, and `make clean` no longer names `dist.first`. `scripts/install-cli.mjs` removed `<prefix>/bin/template-fmt` and `<prefix>/lib/polyspec-template-fmt` before `npm install`, so a failed install left no command: with an `npm` that exits with 1, the second install removed the working first one. It now prepares `<prefix>/lib/.polyspec-template-fmt.next-<pid>`, renames it to the project after `npm install` passes, removes the prepared directory when it fails, and writes the script through a temporary file and a rename. The 3 cases of the new `tests/scripts/atomic-publish.test.mjs` failed on the tree of T19.6 and pass after the change; `tests/scripts/install-cli.test.mjs` and `make docs-verify-idempotent` pass.
- Built what a run reads every time, with builds that do nothing on unchanged inputs (T19.6). `prepare` of `tests/runner/drivers.mjs` built the CLI of a language only when its binary was absent, so the conformance, parity and delimiter runners used a binary built from older sources; it now runs the build of the driver every time: `make build-ts` with `scripts/build-package.mjs`, `go build`, `cargo build` and `composer install`, each of which does nothing when its inputs are unchanged. The new `tests/scripts/drivers.test.mjs` failed on the tree of T19.5, where a present binary was used without a build, and passes after the change. `tests/scripts/install-cli.test.mjs` and `tests/scripts/generated-conformance.test.mjs` read the builds of `make test-scripts`: with `packages/template-language/dist` removed, the install-cli case failed, and with `packages/template-ts/dist` removed, the TypeScript runner case failed. Like `tests/scripts/build-package.test.mjs` (T18.8-2), they build and install the packages they read before their cases, and the generated conformance test also installs the Composer packages of template-php; the same cases pass after the change with the directories removed.
- Installed the Composer packages of template-php before every target that runs PHP with them (T19.5). `conformance-generated-php`, `contract-check`, `benchmark-smoke`, `generated-native-check`, `bench` and `showcase` run `tests/runner/conformance-generated-php.mjs`, the PHP showcase adapter or the generated-call checks, which require `packages/template-php/vendor/autoload.php`, and did not depend on `build-php`: with `vendor` removed, `make generated-native-check` failed with `require(.../packages/template-php/vendor/autoload.php): Failed to open stream: No such file or directory`. The six targets depend on `build-php`, which does nothing when `vendor` matches the lock; with `vendor` removed, `make generated-native-check` installed it and passed. The 9 new cases of `tests/scripts/test-targets.test.mjs`, one for each target that runs PHP with the packages, check that its dry run installs them; 6 failed before the change and the 14 cases pass after it.
- Failed every check that verifies nothing (T19.4). `progress.close` of `scripts/test-progress/progress.mjs` passed a run in which no test ran: `node scripts/run-tests.mjs node -- <file without tests>` printed `✔ node --test: 1 passed, 0 failed`, counting the file, and exited with 0.
