- Added a machine-readable template function contract covering 37 canonical functions, exact arity ranges and four-language AST/generated registry parity. Fixed compiler IR arity validation so zero-argument and variadic functions are checked during lowering.

### 2026-09-11

- Created the repository with the development rules, the top-level documents, the Makefile, the document checker and the execution checklist.

- Added the specification: lexical rules, tag grammar, expressions, data model, functions, runtime, AST with JSON Schema, errors, conformance and a complete example. Reviewed the documents together: loop meta nodes accept further accessors, `{:?}` inside a loop or if-block reports `E_PARSE_ELSEIF_NOT_IN_IF`, wrapped tags (`"{{= x}}"`, `/* {{= x}} */`, `<!-- {{# id}} -->`) and configurable delimiters (engine option and `{% delimiter ;;}` directive) were added, and the tag end is defined by grammar acceptance of the close delimiter.
- Added the conformance runner, the parity runner, the language drivers and the schema checker.
- Verification: `make docs-check` passed with 19 document pairs; `node scripts/check-schema.mjs` passed with zero fixtures.
- Added the TypeScript implementation `@polyspec/template`: source, expression lexer and parser, template parser with standalone lines, wrapped tags and delimiter directive, value model with order-preserving JSON parser, built-in functions, renderer with block registry and limits, map and filesystem loaders, render-only entry, CLI, unit tests and the browser test. Generated the remaining `expected.ast.json` files with the TypeScript parser and validated them against the schema.
- Refined the tag start rule: `/` starts a tag only before the close delimiter and `@` only before `name =`; `{/* */}`, `{/re/}` and `{ @media }` are text. Defined the positions of operator errors as the start of the failing expression, made `+` with a list or map operand `E_RUNTIME_STRINGIFY`, and limited the integer range check to integer literals and integer-typed host values.
- Added the Go implementation `github.com/polyspec/template` and the Rust crate `polyspec-template` with the same module split, unit tests, conformance and expression fixture tests and CLIs. Serialized integer-valued number literals as JSON integers in the Rust AST output. Replaced `cargo` with the `CARGO` variable in the Makefile because GNU Make 3.81 does not apply the exported PATH to directly executed commands.
- Added the PHP implementation `polyspec/template` with the same module split, unit tests, conformance and expression fixture tests and the CLI. `vendor/bin/phpunit` passed 506 tests; `node tests/runner/conformance.mjs --langs ts,php` passed 418 of 418; `node tests/runner/parity.mjs --langs ts,php` reported no divergence.
- Verification of the four implementations: `node tests/runner/conformance.mjs` passed 836 of 836 checks over 209 cases; `node tests/runner/parity.mjs` reported no divergence across ts, go, rust and php.
- Verification: `npm test -w @polyspec/template -- --run` passed 471 tests; `node tests/runner/conformance.mjs --langs ts` passed 209 of 209 cases; `make test-browser` passed; `npm run lint` and `npm run typecheck -w @polyspec/template` passed; `make docs-check` passed with 21 document pairs.
- Optimized the Rust implementation by sharing list and map storage across cloned values, borrowing unchanged text during HTML escaping, preallocating bound maps, borrowing the function environment and using typed AST operators. Updated the PHP extension conversions for shared values.
- Verification: `make doc-coverage` passed; `make check` passed lint, the four package unit suites and 1050 of 1050 conformance checks over 210 cases and five implementations; `make test-ext` built the PHP extension and passed 210 of 210 conformance cases and 235 extension tests; `make bench BENCH_ITERS=3000 BENCH_WARMUP=300` passed its output equality check and wrote the benchmark results.
- Separated assigned variables from template definitions in the render APIs. Renamed the host registry, fixture file and CLI option from `blocks` to `define`, accepted string template paths, and allowed a target ID such as `layout` to resolve through its template definition. Updated all implementations, browser and benchmark drivers, fixtures and documentation.
- Verification: `npm test -w @polyspec/template -- --run` passed 650 tests; `vendor/bin/phpunit` passed 510 tests; `node tests/runner/conformance.mjs --langs ts,go,rust,php` passed 844 of 844 checks over 211 cases; `make test-browser` passed; `make test-ext` passed 211 of 211 conformance cases and 236 extension tests; `make docs-check` passed with 29 document pairs.
- Added an executable example site with four page scenarios: a controller-style layout page, define data and scope precedence, a pre-rendered HTML slot and an empty definition branch. The site records the rendered HTML, cross-language hashes, repeatability checks and per-scenario throughput for ts, go, rust, php and php-ext.

### 2026-09-12

- Passed one mutable render scope through generated Rust templates. Includes now share assignments with their caller, blocks create isolated scopes, loop variables restore the previous binding, and typed template boundaries convert values through the common runtime value model.
- Passed one mutable render scope through generated PHP templates with the same include sharing, block isolation and loop-binding restoration rules.
- Added complete generated PHP conformance. All 211 canonical cases now compile or render to their expected diagnostic or exact HTML; source-block registration, nested loop metadata, spread errors and literal dollar signs use the shared runtime rules.
- Added complete generated Go conformance. All 211 canonical cases now compile or render to their expected diagnostic or exact HTML; generated definition registration, shared include scope, nested loop metadata, dynamic spread and invalid UTF-8 input use the shared runtime rules. Generated loops in all four backends compute invariant size and final-index values once before iteration.
- Added complete generated Rust conformance and the `conformance-all-modes` release command. Rust generated expressions preserve the runtime `Value` model across safe strings, dynamic spread, conditional branches and nested loop metadata, while typed conversion stays at assign, definition and template-input boundaries. The command passes all 1,688 TypeScript, Go, Rust and PHP AST/generated cells.
- Rebuilt the static showcase from the completed product compiler artifacts. Static HTML verification passes parser-backed template tokens, highlighted output HTML, bounded two-axis scrolling for compiled and generated source, all input and template views, and the React island boundary.
- Added an isolated package install verification. It installs the npm tarball, a versioned Go module zip through a local proxy, an extracted Cargo `.crate` and the Composer archive into temporary install projects, then requires AST and generated programs in every language to produce the same 177-byte assign/define page.
- Replaced the diagnostic mode timing with a production-artifact benchmark. Twenty-one independent samples now record compiler, cold process, full render, prepared render, process and RSS medians and P95 values only after all eight language-mode rows produce the same 177-byte output. The generated documentation table and static example page read the same checked result.
- Added a seven-layer commercial release matrix covering generated contracts and documentation, package units, compiler outputs, all 1,688 core mode cells, the PHP extension, positioned recovery and mutation regressions, isolated package installs and browser output, static HTML and a fresh performance smoke run. All 254 specification rules now require fixture or explicit non-fixture evidence, and CI runs the complete gate.
- Added an isolated clean-checkout release gate. PHP package targets install their own locked test dependencies, and the gate installs locked JavaScript dependencies before running the complete matrix from a detached temporary worktree.
- Defined a latest-compatible stable dependency policy with machine-checked pin reasons and removal conditions. Updated compatible test and documentation dependencies, aligned PHP tests with the declared PHP 8.2 minimum, added PHP 8.2 and current-environment CI cells, and added JavaScript and PHP advisory checks to the first release layer. The checked dependency graph reports no known vulnerabilities.

- Separated compilation mode (`ast` or `gen`), compiled-artifact refresh (`dev`, `true` or `false`) and final HTML page-cache TTL (`null` or `0` means permanent) in the four runtimes. Added tests for artifact refresh and page-cache expiration.
- Verified that AST measurement reparses on every render and generated measurement calls generated host-language source. Both paths must produce the same HTML and SHA-256 before timing is recorded.
- Verification: PHP 513 tests, TypeScript 652 tests, Go package tests and Rust package tests passed with zero output divergence between the measured AST and generated paths.

- Added the prepared render contract to the Rust, Go, TypeScript and PHP runtimes. `prepare` binds assign and define data and resolves the parsed target once; repeated `render` calls reuse that state. Updated benchmark drivers, runtime specification, feature status and the static showcase performance description.
- Verification: Rust and Go package checks passed; TypeScript build and PHP syntax checks passed. Full cross-language verification and benchmark rerun remain required before this change is marked complete.

- Added a PHP check of a real Composer path dependency, `php-path-check.mjs` with `php-runner.php`: a temporary project installs `polyspec/template` without a symlink, loads it through Composer, and renders the platform snapshot through `FilesystemLoader`.

- Simplified every example to shared templates, mock assign data, a define registry and the `layout` render target; the site exposes the templates and the mock inputs. Relabeled benchmark P95 as the percentile of sample means.
- Verification after simplification: `make showcase` passed 25 implementation-scenario pairs and recorded 125 benchmark samples. `make showcase-check` passed raw output comparisons and the browser test for shared mock inputs and templates; `make docs-check` passed 30 document pairs.
- Defined the cross-language render contract in RT-43–RT-48 with Mermaid diagrams, a JSON request shape and adapter rules. Standardized every showcase `define.json` entry on the object form, added build-time contract validation, and updated the Go, Rust, TypeScript and JavaScript examples to read the same scenario inputs.
- Made `tools/showcase/adapters/interface.json` the source for adapter types, fields, ownership, operations, errors and state transitions. Added generated declarations and Mermaid diagrams, compile/reflection/runtime contract checks for TypeScript, JavaScript, Go, Rust and PHP, and a failure-recovery proof over every showcase scenario.
- Verification: `make check`, `make showcase`, `make showcase-check` and `make docs-check` passed; the contract gate covered 1055 of 1055 conformance checks over 211 cases and all 25 showcase implementation-scenario pairs.
- Configured the online documentation as a static VitePress artifact with a repository-aware base path, added a GitHub Pages deployment workflow and `make docs-static-check`, and synchronized the publication procedure and execution checklist.

- Simplified showcase template registrations to direct identifier-to-path values, kept object entries only for definition data or finished HTML, and opened the assign JSON and the templates of each scenario on the example page.

- Added the same `PageCache.getOrSet` miss and hit contract to TypeScript, Go, Rust and PHP. A hit returns stored HTML without invoking the render callback; a miss invokes it once and stores the result. Fixed `make clean` so it removes rebuildable outputs without a dangling command continuation.
- Hardened generated-source parity for TypeScript, Go, Rust and PHP. HTML escaping now covers the same five characters, logical operators always return booleans, and empty lists and maps follow the shared truthiness rule. Unsupported generated functions fail in the shared IR instead of surviving as runtime stubs. The build graph now enforces `tpl` to AST to typed host source order, and the compiler contract diagram includes source, manifest, typed program and function signatures.
- Corrected generated-mode status to in progress. The AST runtimes pass 211 conformance cases, while generated execution is limited to five showcase scenarios, the typed compiler accepts only `default`, and a separate showcase generator supplies injected callbacks. Defined the v1 compiler boundary, build-time artifact refresh and the complete internal verification gate before implementation continues.
- Made `AstProgram` and generated `Program` implementations peers behind a delegating `Engine` in TypeScript, Go, Rust and PHP. Removed compile-mode selection and generated-renderer callbacks from runtime engines; generated showcase artifacts now implement `Program` directly. Generated conformance remains limited to the five showcase scenarios until the separate generators are replaced by the product compiler.
- Added parser analysis output containing accepted tag ranges and expression-lexer token ranges. The static example site uses these ranges for template syntax highlighting and bounds compiled artifacts and generated source with two-axis scrolling.
- Added a concrete Go `AstProgram` type so all four public runtimes expose the same `Engine`/`Program`/`AstProgram` ownership structure.
- Extended the compiler manifest with the runtime declaration contract. The interface gate extracts TypeScript, Go and Rust declarations through their language parsers and PHP declarations through Reflection, then checks operations, argument counts, ownership and AST program structure against the manifest.
- Replaced the four duplicated showcase AST directories with one canonical `compiled/ast` graph per scenario. The product AST compiler records source, type and contract digests, publishes the manifest after all files, preserves the previous artifact after a failed build and verifies deployed artifacts without reading template sources under the `false` refresh policy.
- Centralized truthiness, string conversion, escaping, numeric conversion, finite arithmetic checks, equality, ordering, lookup, iteration entries, function calls, limits and positioned errors in `RuntimeBindings` for TypeScript, Go, Rust and PHP. AST evaluators and statement renderers now use that boundary, and the compiler-interface gate rejects missing runtime operations. TypeScript and PHP expression-depth checks now use the configured limit instead of a fixed value.
- Extended the typed IR to preserve every source span, accept explicitly dynamic member, index, spread and loop operations, and retain both built-in and host function signatures. Invalid symbols and function implementation kinds still fail during lowering. Refreshed canonical AST manifests against the current compiler contract digest.
- Bound the initial delimiter pair into canonical AST manifests and source digests so custom-delimiter programs use the same compiler and refresh lifecycle. HTML definitions now discard call-site scope data at lowering because rendered HTML has no target template input.
- Made canonical source graphs follow static include and block dependencies regardless of filename extension. Dynamic manifests now derive and propagate included-template inputs, generated list and map spreads use positioned runtime validation, map keys use canonical string conversion, and generated source blocks update the definition registry with redefinition checks.
- Connected generated TypeScript template functions to the shared render scope: includes preserve assignments, blocks isolate locals, loops restore shadowed bindings, and block root fallback bypasses caller locals. Added a product-compiler conformance gate that compiles and executes all 211 TypeScript generated cases and compares structured diagnostics or exact output.
- Passed the shared render scope through generated Go template and include calls, created isolated block scopes, and restored loop bindings. Generated Go definition binding now emits valid source for programs with no declared definitions.
- Replaced the monolithic host-source generator with one compiler entry and independent TypeScript, Go, Rust and PHP backend modules. Shared IR traversal, naming and recursive type mapping now live outside the language emitters, artifact replacement is atomic, and all twenty current generated programs still compile and render byte-identical HTML.
- Enforced the manifest LanguageBackend contract on the actual compiler modules. Every backend now contributes declarations, runtime support, template functions and the program entry through the same four operations; the compiler owns their ordering. The interface gate parses all four JavaScript modules and its mutation test proves that a missing backend operation fails validation.
- Merged the showcase adapter contract into the compiler interface manifest. Contract declarations, adapter checks, Mermaid generation and canonical artifact digests now read the same design source, and the interface gate rejects a reintroduced duplicate manifest.
- Separated `RuntimeServices` from AST template loading in all four runtimes. Render contexts now depend only on limits and host-function lookup, while AST statement renderers own template loading explicitly. The shared manifest and declaration checks enforce that boundary so generated programs can use `RuntimeBindings` without an AST program dependency.
- Added digest-bound generated artifact manifests and independent `dev`, `true` and `false` refresh behavior with integrity checks and atomic replacement.
- Removed canonical AST ownership from render frames and standardized `RenderFrame` and `RenderScope` fields and operations across TypeScript, Go, Rust and PHP.
- Added a concrete `RuntimeEnvironment` in TypeScript, Go, Rust and PHP as the common owner of resource limits and host functions. AST programs now delegate runtime services to it, the generated-program contract requires the same environment, and parser/Reflection checks reject field, operation, signature or ownership drift.
- Made the product compiler emit concrete `GeneratedProgram` implementations for TypeScript, Go, Rust and PHP. Package-level integration checks compile and execute all five current scenarios through those programs and require byte-identical HTML.
- Added compiler implementation digests to schema 3 AST and generated artifact manifests. The `true` refresh policy now rebuilds after parser or backend changes, while `false` verifies deployed files without reading source, type, contract or compiler inputs.
- Replaced the showcase-only renderer generator with product compiler artifacts. TypeScript, JavaScript, Go, Rust and PHP adapters now select a scenario-specific `GeneratedProgram`; Go artifacts use isolated packages and JavaScript delivery artifacts are compiled from the TypeScript backend output.
- Added an explicit dynamic-root type contract to the compiler IR. A dynamic root accepts undeclared input names as optional runtime values while preserving declared template inputs, records, definitions and function signatures; fixed `Assign` roots continue to reject undeclared fields.
- Preserved each template's source-line byte index in the canonical artifact and typed IR so generated runtime errors can retain exact source positions without loading template source.
- Moved unary and eager binary value semantics into the common `RuntimeBindings` contract in all four runtimes. AST evaluators now retain only short-circuit control flow and delegate the resulting operation, preventing generated backends from defining a second arithmetic, comparison or membership model.
- Connected TypeScript generated programs to the package `RenderContext` and `RuntimeBindings`. Direct template functions now use shared value operations, function dispatch, source positions, render-chain checks, iteration limits and the UTF-8 output limiter; generation checks reject duplicated helper semantics.
- Connected PHP generated programs to the package `Context` and `RuntimeBindings`. Generated template calls now preserve source positions, render-chain and output limits, while typed maps remain `MapValue` values and generation checks reject duplicated value-operation helpers.
- Connected Go generated programs to `render.Context` and `render.RuntimeBindings`. Generated execution now keeps positioned template errors intact, enforces output, depth and iteration limits, preserves lazy expression branches, and rejects the previous reflection-based value semantics.
- Connected Rust generated programs to `RenderContext` and `RuntimeBindings`. Generated template functions now return structured errors directly, restore source line indexes from artifacts, enforce shared limits and preserve lazy branches without duplicated truthiness, membership or escaping implementations.
- Corrected typed IR nullability so member and index result type sources agree with their optional flag; Go host binding now recursively accepts optional scalar pointers exposed by those generated types.
- Added deterministic dynamic type-manifest derivation for all 189 parseable conformance cases, covering referenced functions, definition targets and block inputs without inferring types from sample data.
